Method: 作者综合分析了TrustNLP研讨会自2021年以来的全部144篇论文，基于已有框架（TrustLLM、DecodingTrust）将其归入六个信任维度进行分类编码，并进行了跨会议对比（ACL、NAACL、EACL、EMNLP约2000篇论文），以检验TrustNLP主题分布的领域代表性。

Result: 研究发现：truthfulness是增长最快的维度（2021-2022年缺失，到2025-2026年占37%）；公平性是最持续的主题；可解释性呈U形轨迹（事后方法衰退后经机制可解释性在2026年复苏）。高影响力聊天模型的出现同时激活了所有信任维度，后续模型代际则聚焦于真实性和安全对齐。跨会议对比显示TrustNLP的主题分布与领域平均水平高度一致。

Conclusion: 信任NLP研究已从静态模型的事后可解释性转向生成式系统的机制理解和主动控制。作者提出四个结构性洞察，并给出了社区可行的发展建议，强调TrustNLP的研究分布能代表整个领域的发展趋势。

Abstract: The Workshop on Trustworthy Natural Language Processing (TrustNLP), co-located with major ACL conferences since 2021, has grown from 8 proceedings papers to 41 over six editions, documenting a field-wide transition from post-hoc interpretability of static models to mechanistic understanding and proactive control of generative systems. We synthesize insights from all 144 proceedings papers, classifying them along six trust dimensions grounded in established frameworks (TrustLLM, DecodingTrust). We observe co-occurrences with capability emergence. The release of the first high-impact chat models activated all trust dimensions simultaneously, while subsequent model generations shifted focus toward truthfulness and safety alignment. Analysis from the classification study reveals that truthfulness is the fastest-growing dimension (absent in 2021-2022, comprising 37% of papers by 2025-2026), fairness remains the most consistent theme, and explainability exhibits a U-shaped trajectory; declining as post-hoc methods lost relevance but resurging in 2026 through mechanistic interpretability. A cross-venue comparison with ACL, NAACL, EACL, and EMNLP (~2K papers) in the same period shows that TrustNLP's topical distribution closely follows the field average. We identify four structural insights and conclude with actionable directions for the research community.

</details>


### [175] [ConVAWG: A Retrieval-Grounded Framework for Controlled Synthetic Dialogue Generation in Violence Against Women and Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11200)
*Chen Lyu,Xingwei Tan,Simon Cullen,Shelley Wilson,Lois Arthurs,Arshad Jhumka,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConVAWG框架，用于生成CPS对齐的合成家庭暴力（VAWG）多轮对话数据集。该框架基于检索增强，从人物种子、英国统计局人口统计模式、官方犯罪定义及检索到的家庭凶杀案审查案例构建场景，转换为分层事件时间线，生成多场景角色扮演对话，并通过激活导向的毒性控制技术对适当的语句进行调整。研究发布了200个场景、超6000个多轮对话事件，并经过广泛的人类评估、LLM评估、消融实验和下游任务验证，展示了强大的对话质量和领域保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中亲密关系暴力等敏感对话数据难以获取、发布和标注，受隐私和法律约束限制。现有工作主要聚焦于句子级在线滥用的毒性检测，缺乏将虐待建模为关系性和时间展开现象的对话级数据集。

Method: ConVAWG框架包含四个关键组件：(1) 从人物种子、英国统计局人口统计模式、官方犯罪定义和检索到的家庭凶杀案审查案例构建场景；(2) 将场景转化为分层事件时间线；(3) 生成多场景角色扮演对话；(4) 应用激活导向的毒性控制技术对适当语句进行调控。

Result: 发布了200个场景、超过6000个多轮对话事件的数据集，包含丰富的场景级、事件级和轮次级元数据。通过人类评估、LLM-as-Judge评估、消融实验和下游任务验证，证明了生成的对话具有高质量和强领域保真度。

Conclusion: ConVAWG填补了将虐待建模为多轮对话现象的空白，为研究动态性、关系性的敏感领域对话提供了可扩展的合成数据生成方案，且生成的数据在质量和领域保真度上均表现优异。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue generation offers a way to study conversational dynamics in sensitive domains where real data are difficult to access, release, or annotate. The underlying abuse may occur online or offline: threats and coercion can appear directly in messages, while behaviours such as surveillance, isolation, stalking, and physical violence may be planned, disclosed, or referred to conversationally. Privacy and legal constraints make it difficult the release of large-scale real conversation datasets; existing work has mostly focused on sentence-level toxicity of online abuses, leaving a gap in modelling abuse as a relational and temporally unfolding phenomenon. In this work, we focus on modelling Violence Against Women and Girls (VAWG) scenarios as multi-turn dialogues. We introduce ConVAWG, a retrieval-grounded framework for generating CPS-aligned synthetic VAWG chat dialogues. ConVAWG builds scenarios from persona seeds, demographic patterns reported by the UK Office for National Statistics, official crime definitions, and retrieved Domestic Homicide Review cases; converts them into hierarchical event timelines; generates multi-scene role-play dialogues; and applies targeted activation-steered toxicity control to appropriate utterances. We release over 6,000 multi-turn dialogue events across 200 scenarios with rich scenario-, event-, and turn-level metadata. Extensive human evaluation, LLM-as-Judge assessment, ablations, and downstream tasks show strong dialogue quality and domain fidelity.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Spatio-Temporal Scheduling for Robust and Efficient Multi-Transmitter Wireless Power Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10391)
*Yuna Sawada,Shino Shiraki,Nobuyoshi Kikuma,Takahiro Matsuda,Takefumi Hiraguri,Kazuki Maruta,Tomotaka Kimura*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多用户无线能量传输（WPT）系统中的时空调度方案，通过协调多发射机进行波束成形，结合时分（TD）和空分（SD）调度，在时变信道条件下实现既高效又稳健的功率传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时分WPT（TD-WPT）虽然能利用整流天线的非线性特性，但依赖单一位置的定向传输，在时变信道条件下鲁棒性不足。因此需要探索能够兼顾效率和鲁棒性的调度方案。

Method: 提出一种协调多发射机WPT系统中的时空调度方案，利用多个发射机提高对信道变化的鲁棒性，并通过发射机间的协调波束成形来利用簇间干扰，同时结合时分和空分调度策略。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方案即使在阴影（shadowing）条件下也能实现鲁棒且高效的功率供应，验证了所提方案的潜力。

Conclusion: 协调多发射机的时空调度方案能够在保证能量传输效率的同时，增强系统对时变信道的鲁棒性，为多用户WPT提供了一种更稳健的功率传输方案。

Abstract: In multi-user wireless power transfer (WPT), scheduling schemes that determine the allocation of transmission resources among receivers play a crucial role in improving power transfer efficiency. Scheduling can be classified into time-division (TD) and space-division (SD) schemes, with the transmission order and direction designed to control when and to whom power is delivered. TD-WPT can exploit the nonlinear characteristics of rectennas by concentrating power in time; however, a system relying on highly directional transmission from a single location reduces robustness under time-varying channel conditions. This study investigated the effectiveness of spatio-temporal scheduling in coordinated multi-transmitter WPT systems. By employing multiple transmitters, the proposed method is robust against channel variations in delivering power. Moreover, coordinated beamforming among transmitters exploits inter-cluster interference. Simulation results demonstrate the potential of the proposed scheme for robust and efficient power supply, even under shadowing conditions.

</details>


### [177] [Link-adaptive digital twin for robust physical-layer modeling in hybrid-amplified ultra-wideband optical networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10517)
*Xiaoxuan Gao,Rentao Gu,Yingchun Wang,Xinyi Liu,Junshi Gao,Yuefeng Ji*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种链路自适应数字孪生（LA-DT）方法，用于混合放大超宽带链路的物理层建模，通过将GSNR建模分解为ASE、NLI和信号功率预测，采用带线性调制层（LMLs）的新型神经架构和域判别器引导的少样本微调，实现了跨场景的高精度GSNR估计和快速适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超宽带系统中受激拉曼散射（ISRS）效应加剧，使得精确物理层建模对可靠性和容量优化至关重要。现有方法在泛化能力和速度上存在局限，尤其是面对EDFA异质性和多样链路场景时难以兼顾精度与适应性。

Method: 1) 将GSNR建模任务分解为EDFA入口前的ASE、NLI和信号功率三个关键预测；2) 开发三个专用DT模型，采用带线性调制层（LMLs）的新型神经架构；3) 使用三个域判别器指导LMLs在有限数据下进行少样本微调，以快速适应未见场景；4) 显式考虑拉曼放大器（RA）插入损耗，提升实际部署可靠性。

Result: 在35个场景中，LA-DT将NLI、ASE和信号功率预测的RMSE降至0.151、0.111和0.113 dBm，相比基线分别提升56.0%、58.4%和52.7%；平均GSNR估计RMSE为0.114 dBm（提升55.8%）。在12个未见场景中，仅用每场景20个样本进行少样本微调，平均GSNR RMSE达到0.159 dB，展现出强适应性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LA-DT通过任务分解、专用神经架构和少样本微调机制，有效解决了混合放大超宽带链路建模中的泛化性和速度问题，在高精度GSNR估计、跨场景适应性和实际部署可靠性方面均取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Accurate physical-layer modeling is increasingly essential for reliable ultra-wideband operation and capacity optimization, especially under the intensified inter-channel stimulated Raman scattering (ISRS) effect. This paper proposes the link-adaptive digital twin (LA-DT) for hybrid-amplified ultra-wideband links to overcome the generalization and speed limitations of existing methods, achieving accurate modeling and robust generalized signal-to-noise ratio (GSNR) estimation across diverse links. First, to address EDFA heterogeneity, the GSNR modeling task is decomposed into three key power predictions: ASE, NLI, and signal powers before EDFA entry. Second, to enhance cross-scenario generalization, three dedicated DT models are developed using a novel neural architecture with linear modulation layers (LMLs). Third, for rapid adaptation to unseen scenarios with limited data, three domain discriminators guide few-shot fine-tuning of the LMLs. Fourth, the LA-DT explicitly accounts for Raman amplifier (RA) insertion loss, improving practical deployment reliability. Results across 35 scenarios show that LA-DT reduces RMSE for NLI, ASE, and signal power predictions to 0.151, 0.111, and 0.113 dBm with improvements of 56.0%, 58.4%, and 52.7% over the baseline,and achieves an average GSNR estimation RMSE of 0.114 dBm (55.8% improvement). For 12 unseen scenarios, the LA-DT maintains high accuracy through few-shot fine-tuning with only 20 samples per scenario, achieving an average GSNR RMSE of 0.159 dB and demonstrating strong adaptability and robustness.

</details>


### [178] [Benchmarking LLM-Guided Control-Plane Policies for Backend Fault Isolation in HAProxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10532)
*Aman Chauhan,Vishnu Pendyala*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究用大语言模型（LLM）替代静态负载均衡策略，通过读取HAProxy和Prometheus遥测数据，每10秒检测一次故障后端，并通过有防护的API调用隔离故障服务器。在包含约三分之一持续结构故障的异构集群基准测试中，作者评估了15个开放权重模型（0.35B至35B参数，5个家族），发现约3B活跃参数存在能力阈值：低于该阈值时LLM策略不可靠甚至比无策略更差；高于该阈值时所有模型均能达到比静态基线减少约88%的5xx错误。但可用性提升伴随代价：故障隔离使尾部延迟膨胀2.6-2.8倍，启用推理模式导致令牌消耗增加约十倍。最优运行点是阈值之上的模型使用最便宜的非推理模式，并包裹在确定性防护栏内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态负载均衡器无法应对"降级而非宕机"的后端故障，会持续向返回HTTP 500的服务器路由流量直到人工介入。作者探索LLM能否替代静态路由策略，实现自动化的故障检测与隔离。

Method: 设计了一个可复现基准测试：在一群异构服务器中构建约三分之一的持续结构故障，LLM每10秒读取HAProxy和Prometheus遥测数据，通过有防护的HAProxy Data Plane API调用隔离故障服务器。系统性地扫描了15个开放权重模型，涵盖5个家族（0.35B到35B参数）、不同架构（稠密、混合专家、高效稀疏）、推理模式、3到9台后端的集群规模和两种路由算法，共240次运行。

Result: 发现约3B活跃参数处存在能力阈值。低于该阈值，LLM策略通常不可靠甚至比无策略更差；高于该阈值，无论架构如何，所有模型都接近88%的5xx错误减少。阈值是近似的：Gemma 4 E2B以2B活跃参数跨过阈值，而稠密的3B Granite 4.0 Micro未达到。同时发现成本：故障排空将尾部延迟膨胀2.6-2.8倍，推理模式使令牌消耗增加约十倍并超过控制间隔，降低有效性。

Conclusion: 高阶最优运行点是使用阈值之上的LLM模型，采用最便宜的非推理模式，并包裹在确定性防护栏（guardrails）内。这既实现了接近88%的5xx错误减少，又避免了推理模式的额外开销。

Abstract: Static load balancers cannot mitigate a backend that is degraded rather than down: round-robin and least-connections keep routing traffic to a server returning HTTP 500s until an operator intervenes. We ask whether a Large Language Model can replace the static routing policy itself, reading HAProxy and Prometheus telemetry every 10 seconds and isolating faulty servers through guardrailed calls to the HAProxy Data Plane API. On a reproducible benchmark with a persistent structural fault built into roughly one-third of a heterogeneous fleet, we sweep 15 open-weight models across five families (0.35B to 35B total parameters; dense, mixture-of-experts, and efficient-sparse architectures), reasoning modes, fleet scales of 3 to 9 backends, and two routing algorithms, totaling 240 runs. We find a capability threshold near 3B active parameters. Below it, LLM policies are typically unreliable and sometimes worse than no policy; above it, every model, regardless of architecture, saturates near an 88% reduction in client-perceived 5xx errors over the static baseline. The threshold is approximate: Gemma 4 E2B clears it with 2B active parameters, while the dense 3B Granite 4.0 Micro does not. The availability gain has costs. Draining concentrates load onto surviving servers, inflating tail latency 2.6 to 2.8 times, and enabling reasoning multiplies token spend roughly tenfold, overrunning the control interval and degrading effectiveness. The efficient operating point is a supra-threshold model in its cheapest non-reasoning mode, wrapped inside deterministic guardrails.

</details>


### [179] [An Asynchronous Triggered MAC Protocol for Underwater Acoustic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10533)
*Bingwen Huangfu,Jiani Guo,Shanshan Song,Nan Sun,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出AT-MAC协议，将水下MAC设计从同步时隙范式转向异步调度范式，通过触发式时隙和增强的多智能体深度强化学习，实现无需时间同步的异步变长时隙传输，并配备负载感知公平性保护机制，在多种场景下展现出优越性与适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TDMA水下MAC协议依赖同步且固定长度的时隙范式，但这在水声网络中带来巨大的时钟同步开销（因声速低且多变），且难以提升调度灵活性。虽然一些协议尝试调整时隙长度，仍受限于信道利用率与调度复杂度之间的权衡。

Method: AT-MAC采用触发式时隙范式，无需时间同步，将传输调度从固定时间线中解耦，支持异步、变长时隙以适应长且多样的传播延迟。同时，通过增强多智能体深度强化学习处理异步交互，实现部分观测下的高效协调信道接入；并设计负载感知公平性保护机制，仅借助本地侦听即可推断全网公平状态，从而指导自适应调度修正。

Result: 基于轨迹和板载实验验证了AT-MAC的可行性和计算实用性，广泛仿真结果表明其在多种场景和流量条件下具有优越性和适应性。

Conclusion: AT-MAC通过范式转换（同步时隙→异步调度）有效解决了水下网络中的时钟同步开销和调度灵活性受限问题，为水下MAC协议设计提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Time Division Multiple Access (TDMA)-based Medium Access Control (MAC) protocols have proven their practicality through extensive field trials in Underwater Acoustic Networks (UANs), attributable to their hardware-agnostic and easily implementable properties. Most existing protocols rely on a synchronized and fixed-length slot paradigm to mitigate channel contention and facilitate orderly transmissions. However, this paradigm imposes significant clock synchronization overhead in UANs with low and variable sound speed and struggles to improve scheduling flexibility. Although some protocols attempt to refine this slot paradigm (adjust the slot length to improve channel reuse efficiency or scheduling frequency), they are still constrained by the trade-off between channel utilization and scheduling complexity. To this end, this paper advocates a paradigm shift in underwater MAC design, transitioning from synchronized slot to asynchronous scheduling, and realizes it through an Asynchronous Triggered MAC (AT-MAC). AT-MAC introduces a triggered slot paradigm without time synchroniza?tion, decoupling transmission scheduling from a rigid timeline and enabling asynchronous, variable-length slots to accommodate the long and diverse propagation delays. To power this slot paradigm, AT-MAC augments conventional Multi-Agent Deep Reinforcement Learning to handle asynchronous interaction, achieving efficient coordinated channel access under partial ob?servations. It further devises a load-aware fairness guard mech?anism to enable network-wide fairness status inference solely through local overhearing, thereby guiding adaptive scheduling correction to maintain fairness. Trace-based and on-board ex?periments validate the feasibility and computational practicality of AT-MAC. Extensive simulation results further demonstrate its superiority and adaptability across various scenarios and traffic conditions.

</details>


### [180] [ImpactHO: Importance-Aware KV Cache Transfer for Multi-User Edge LLM Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10545)
*Minwoo Kim,Soochang Song,Namyoon Lee,Bang Chul Jung,Yongjune Kim*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向边缘LLM切换场景的KV缓存传输分配方法。当用户在边缘节点间切换时，需传输KV缓存以保证推理连续性，但并发切换会使回程链路饱和。作者按重要性对每个用户的KV缓存排序，仅传输信息量最高的部分，将token级稀疏性转化为通信节省，并将问题建模为多用户回程分配优化问题以最大化平均准确率。通过闭式加权注水求解，在500ms传输窗口内达到93.7%以上平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘LLM在用户跨节点切换时需要传输KV缓存以保持推理连续性，但并发切换会使回程链路饱和，无法在移动限制的传输窗口内完成全部缓存传输。传统方法将所有缓存条目等权分配带宽，忽略了不同缓存条目的重要性差异，效率低下。

Method: 1) 按重要性对每个用户的KV缓存排序，仅传输信息量最高的部分；2) 将传输问题建模为多用户回程带宽分配问题，以平均准确率最大化为目标；3) 使用sigmoid函数拟合部分缓存准确率作为效用函数（在RULER基准上R²>0.99）；4) 利用重要性排序使准确率曲线凹区覆盖几乎整个缓存，保证每个时隙分配问题为凸；5) 通过闭式加权注水（water-filling）方法求解最优解，该解推广了信息论注水方法，支持在线调度。

Result: 所提分配器在500ms传输窗口内达到93.7%以上的平均准确率，仅比全缓存上限低0.5个百分点，并达到理想预言上限（clairvoyant upper bound）的98.2-99.5%。

Conclusion: 通过将token级稀疏性转化为通信节省，并利用闭式加权注水分配方法，可以在回程受限的条件下近乎实现全缓存传输的推理准确率，为边缘LLM切换场景提供了高效的缓存传输调度方案。

Abstract: Edge LLMs must preserve inference continuity when a user hands over between edge nodes, requiring key-value (KV) cache transfer to the target node. However, simultaneous handovers saturate the backhaul, preventing full cache delivery within the mobility-imposed transfer window. Rather than allocating bandwidth as if all cache entries were equally valuable, we order each user's KV cache by importance and transmit only its most informative fraction, turning token-level sparsity into communication savings. We cast the transfer as a multi-user backhaul allocation problem that maximizes average accuracy across users. Each user's partial-cache accuracy serves as its utility: a sigmoid that fits measurements on the RULER benchmark with $R^2>0.99$ across models and context lengths. Because importance ordering front-loads the high-value entries, the concave region of the accuracy curve spans nearly the entire cache. Our proposed allocator keeps served users within this region, making each per-slot allocation problem convex. The optimum is derived via a closed-form weighted water-filling solution that generalizes information-theoretic water-filling and enables online scheduling. The proposed allocator attains over 93.7% average accuracy in a 500ms transfer window, within 0.5pp of the full-cache ceiling, and reaches 98.2-99.5% of a clairvoyant upper bound.

</details>


### [181] [Sensing in Low-altitude Wireless Networks: Systems, Techniques, and Developments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10555)
*Zihao Tao,Yiming Zhao,Hongtao Zhao,Zijun Gong,Ying Cui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了面向低空无线网络（LAWN）的感知技术，从系统框架、核心技术、研究趋势三个维度展开，并提供了一个模型与数据驱动的多模态实时空中目标感知案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空空域具有高度动态性和安全关键性，感知是低空无线网络不可或缺的组成部分。尽管现有感知技术已在多种范式下被广泛研究，但最先进的感知方案与LAWN的实际感知需求之间仍存在显著不匹配，需要系统的综述来填补这一研究空白。

Method: 该综述从三个维度系统梳理LAWN感知：(1) 系统框架分析，涵盖概念、服务与任务、节点与目标、场景；(2) 从传播介质、协作方式、方法论和模态四个角度对现有感知技术进行比较分析，评估其优缺点；(3) 总结未来研究方向，包括非协作与协作感知、模型驱动与数据驱动感知、模型与数据驱动的多模态感知，并给出一个多模态实时空中目标感知的案例研究。

Result: 该文章提供了比现有LAWN或感知综述更全面、更有针对性的综述，完全专注于LAWN感知，并通过案例研究验证了模型与数据驱动多模态方法在实时空中目标感知中的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了现有研究在LAWN感知系统性综述方面的空白，为未来可部署的LAWN感知系统指明了研究方向，强调了模型驱动与数据驱动相结合的多模态感知作为重要发展趋势。

Abstract: The highly dynamic and safety-critical characteristics of low-altitude airspace render sensing an indispensable component of low-altitude wireless networks (LAWN). Although sensing techniques have been extensively studied under diverse paradigms, a prominent mismatch persists between state-of-the-art sensing schemes and the practical sensing demands of LAWN. To fill this research gap, this article systematically reviews LAWN-oriented sensing from the dimensions of system framework, core technologies, and research trends. Specifically, we first analyze the sensing system framework, covering concepts, services and tasks, nodes and targets, and scenarios for LAWN sensing. Next, we conduct a comparative analysis of existing sensing techniques from the perspectives of propagation medium, cooperation, methodology, and modality, analyzing their advantages and limitations. Then, we summarize promising future research directions for deployable LAWN sensing systems, covering non-cooperative and cooperative sensing, model-driven and data-driven sensing, and model-and-data-driven multi-modal sensing. Finally, we present a case study of a model-and-data-driven multi-modal method for real-time aerial target sensing. Compared with existing surveys on LAWN or sensing, this article delivers a more comprehensive, targeted review exclusively centered on LAWN sensing.

</details>


### [182] [Measuring the End-to-End Resilience of Application Deployments in Real-World Communication Networks with DRACO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10611)
*Leon Janzen,Matthias Hollick*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DRACO框架，用于建模通信网络并测量应用部署的端到端韧性（resilience），能够在任意通信网络上配置应用部署并评估其可用性、鲁棒性和韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信网络是ICT中的关键基础设施，但运营商很少公开其信息，导致难以测量应用部署的端到端韧性。因为测量需要猜测拓扑结构及涉及系统的可用性，这带来了挑战。

Method: 提出了DRACO框架，一个用于建模通信网络和测量应用部署端到端韧性的框架。它支持在任意通信网络上配置应用部署，并评估其可用性（availability）、鲁棒性（robustness）和韧性（resilience）。

Result: 通过建模德国和法国的全国性应用部署来证明DRACO的功能范围，展示了其与多种公开数据源和合成数据的兼容性。

Conclusion: DRACO能够有效利用公开和合成数据源来评估通信网络的应用部署韧性，为在信息不透明的真实网络中测量端到端韧性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Billions of users take Internet connectivity for granted and use it daily to access applications deployed anywhere around the globe. What users perceive as a seamless connection between their end device and the application server involves a complex system of systems operated by various actors. Failures in any of these systems disrupt user-to-application connectivity if the communication network is not resilient. Since communication networks are a critical infrastructure as part of ICT, real-world operators rarely disclose information about them. This makes it difficult to measure the end-to-end resilience of deployments, because it requires guessing topologies and the availability of the involved systems.
  This paper presents DRACO, a framework for modeling communication networks and measuring end-to-end resilience of application deployments. It enables configuring application deployments across arbitrary communication networks and evaluating their availability, robustness, and resilience. We demonstrate the functional scope of DRACO by modeling nationwide application deployments in Germany and France, highlighting its compatibility with a wide variety of public data sources and synthetic data.

</details>


### [183] [User Satisfaction-Aware Resource Allocation with Prospect-Theoretic Utility for Multimedia Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10625)
*Manoj Kumar S,Pranjal Varshney,Avhishek Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于前景理论（prospect theory）的流媒体资源分配新指标与策略。该指标同时捕捉平均吞吐量以及吞吐量变化对用户满意度的不对称影响（减少比增加更令人不满），并据此设计了一种在资源从充裕转为稀缺时减少资源骤降的分配策略。仿真表明该策略优于现有公平分配策略，同时提出了快速数值方法来确定在实际部署中需要动态调整的最优阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的流媒体资源分配策略主要关注吞吐量、公平性和延迟等指标，但用户满意度深受吞吐量变化的影响。现有工作虽然考虑了吞吐量变化，却忽略了行为经济学中前景理论揭示的关键人类行为特征：等量吞吐量下降带来的不满大于等量上升带来的满足（损失厌恶）。作者旨在将这一洞见引入资源分配领域。

Method: 1) 提出一个通用的效用（指标），同时捕捉平均吞吐量和吞吐量变化的不对称效应（基于前景理论）。2) 提出一种资源分配策略，在系统从资源充裕转向资源稀缺状态时减少分配资源的急剧下降，同时尊重不同用户类别的优先级。3) 基于多队列系统的理论分析，提出快速数值方法来求解最优阈值，适用于实际部署中网络状态频繁变化的场景。

Result: 大量仿真表明该策略优于现有的公平分配策略。同时，所提出的快速数值方法能够有效确定最优阈值，适用于实际网络部署。

Conclusion: 将前景理论中的损失厌恶概念引入资源分配，可以显著提升用户满意度，超越传统公平分配策略。通过快速数值方法解决了最优阈值随网络实例动态变化的实际部署问题，为流媒体资源分配提供了一种更具用户感知合理性的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional resource allocation policies for multimedia streaming have primarily targeted metrics such as throughput, fairness and delay. However, user satisfaction is known to be strongly influenced by variations in throughput. Although some recent works have considered throughput variations, they overlooked a well-known aspect of human behavior that has reshaped behavioral economics over the past few decades: the same amount of decrease in throughput causes greater annoyance than the satisfaction caused by the increase. Motivated by this insight from the broad field of economics, popularly known as prospect theory, we propose a generic utility (metric) that captures both the average throughput and the asymmetric effect of throughput variations on user satisfaction. Next, we propose a resource allocation policy that reduces abrupt decreases in allocated resources when the system transitions from a resource-rich to resource-scarce state while respecting the priorities of different classes of users. Extensive simulations show that this policy outperforms the existing fair allocation policies. However, the choice of the optimal threshold depends on the network instance, which, in practice, may change frequently. Hence, building on the theoretical analysis of multi-queue systems, we propose fast numerical methods for determining the optimal threshold, which can be used in field deployments.

</details>


### [184] [Conversational Orchestration for Organic 6G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10714)
*Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）驱动的领域智能体的轻量级去中心化对话式编排框架，用于Organic 6G网络（网络之网络）。每个领域保持自治，智能体通过工具观察本地状态、闭环推理，并通过与数据面耦合对齐的A2A覆盖网络与邻居交换摘要。通过类似路由的可达性公告实现快速可行放置，通过事件驱动的请求与协商实现安全重优化、扩缩容和迁移。采用带验证器自验证的紧凑推理模型以满足实时约束，并通过仿真验证了可控的近线性控制面开销和稳健的决策质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域编排方案依赖重型集成架构、多层协调器和深度遥测管道，阻碍了可部署性并放大协调开销。在Organic 6G愿景中，服务提供需要在独立管理的域之间简单运营、可扩展、且能在域频繁加入/离开（域churn）时保持灵活，现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: 1）提出LLM驱动的领域智能体框架，每个域自治运行；2）智能体通过工具观察本地状态、闭环推理并与邻居交换摘要（A2A覆盖网络与数据面耦合对齐）；3）通过周期性、类路由的可达性公告（延迟、瓶颈带宽、计算容量）实现快速可行放置；4）通过事件驱动的请求与协商处理重优化、扩缩容和迁移；5）部署带验证器自验证的紧凑推理模型，并通过影子更新在线定期精炼以满足实时约束。

Result: 仿真结果显示：控制面开销随域规模扩展和域加入呈近线性且可控；决策质量稳健，包括目标变化后的恢复能力。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了LLM驱动代理编排在Organic 6G中的可行性，未来研究方向包括原则性、安全性和不确定性感知的智能体编排。

Abstract: The Organic 6G vision of a network of networks spanning an edge-cloud continuum complemented by non-terrestrial resources requires, to realize its promise, service provisioning that is simple to operate, scalable across independently administered domains, and agile under domain churn (i.e., domains dynamically joining and leaving). Despite advances in cross-domain orchestration, many proposals rely on heavy integration fabrics, multi-layer coordinators, and deep telemetry pipelines that hinder deployability and amplify coordination overhead. We propose a lightweight, decentralized conversational orchestration framework based on Large Language Model (LLM)-driven domain agents. Each domain remains autonomous: an agent observes local state via tools, reasons in a closed loop, and exchanges summaries with neighboring agents over an Agent-to-Agent (A2A) overlay aligned with data-plane coupling. Fast feasible placement is enabled by periodic, routing-like dissemination of reachability advertisements (latency, bottleneck bandwidth, and compute capacity), while safe re-optimization, scaling, and migration are handled through event-driven requests and negotiation. To meet real-time constraints, we deploy a compact reasoning model trained with verifier-based self-verification and periodically refined online via shadow updates. Simulations show manageable, near-linear control-plane overhead as domains scale and during domain joins, and robust decision quality, including recovery after objective changes. We close by outlining future research directions for principled, secure, and uncertainty-aware agentic orchestration in Organic 6G.

</details>


### [185] [Media-over-Multipath-QUIC for Realtime Video Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10741)
*Tanya Shreedhar,Zuji Zhou,Nitinder Mohan,Fernando Kuipers*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: MoMQ是一个MoQT扩展，它将媒体对象的元数据转化为路径调度决策，使实时视频在多路径传输中获得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多路径传输（WiFi、蜂窝、卫星）对实时视频几乎没有增益，因为调度器只能看到字节，无法区分关键帧和增强帧。问题的限制因素不是路径多样性不足，而是缺乏应用命名传输所不可见内容的通道。

Method: 利用MoQT（已在生产CDN上部署）中媒体对象携带的元数据，在边缘中继处安装声明式规则，匹配对象元数据并针对标记路径表达交付偏好。中继机械地执行规则，无需理解视频逻辑。

Result: 在Starlink和WiFi的实时测试平台上，四条规则将P99.9帧完成时间从384.7ms降至114.1ms，所需播放缓冲减少61.5%。MoMQ是唯一满足150ms交互延迟目标的方法。跨两国中继和两大洲用户验证了规则的普适性。

Conclusion: MoMQ证明了将应用语义元数据转化为路径决策能够显著提升多路径传输的实时视频质量，且规则在路径不相交时保持通用性和优势。

Abstract: Multipath transports place a client's WiFi, cellular, and satellite networks under one connection, yet real-time video gains little from them. The scheduler that assigns packets to paths sees only bytes, so it cannot tell a keyframe that anchors a second of video from an enhancement frame whose loss costs one image. We show that the limiting factor is not a shortage of path diversity but the absence of a channel through which the application can name what the transport cannot see. Media over QUIC Transport (MoQT), already deployed on production CDNs, carries media as named Objects, and relays forward each Object's metadata without interpreting it. The knowledge the scheduler lacks therefore already flows through the subscriber's relay. We present MoMQ, a MoQT extension that turns this metadata into path decisions. Applications and relay operators install declarative rules at the edge relay that match Object metadata and express delivery preferences against labeled paths. The relay evaluates the rules mechanically, so it acts on video semantics while containing no video logic. On a live testbed spanning Starlink and WiFi, four rules cut P99.9 frame completion time from 384.7 ms under the best transport-only scheduler to 114.1 ms and reduce the required playback buffer by 61.5%. MoMQ is the only configuration that meets the 150 ms interactive latency target. Across relays in two countries and subscribers on two continents, the same rules apply unchanged and retain their advantage wherever the paths remain disjoint.

</details>


### [186] [Energy-Efficient Joint Optimization of VLC and Fog Computing Resources Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10752)
*Wafaa B. M. Fadlelmula,Sanaa Hamid Mohamed,Taisir E. H. El-Gorashi,Jaafar M. H. Elmirghani*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了室内可见光通信（VLC）与无源光网络（PON）回传相结合的组网架构，通过混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型联合优化AP选择、波长分配和雾计算资源分配，以最小化处理和网络总功耗。实验表明，与静态预配置方案相比，联合优化能降低平均总功耗，主要归因于处理功耗的减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内网络需要同时考虑通信（VLC）和计算（雾计算）资源的能耗问题，而传统方法将AP选择、波长分配与雾资源放置独立优化，无法实现全局最优的能效。因此需要联合优化方案来降低网络整体功耗。

Method: 提出一个混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，联合优化三个维度：（1）VLC接入点（AP）的选择；（2）PON回传网络中的波长分配；（3）雾计算资源的分配，以满足用户请求的同时最小化处理和网络功耗。

Result: 与基于最大化信干噪比（SINR）且独立于雾资源放置的静态预配置方案相比，联合优化实现了更低的平均总功耗，其中处理功耗的降低是主要贡献因素。

Conclusion: 联合优化的节能优势源于两方面：允许AP在用户间共享，以及选择能效更高且仍能满足服务质量（QoS）要求的波长。这验证了通信与计算资源联合优化的必要性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider an indoor networking architecture in which visible light communication (VLC) provides wireless connectivity between users and distributed fog computing resources and an energy-efficient passive optical network (PON)- based backhaul interconnects the VLC access points (APs). We investigate the joint optimization of the allocation of VLC and fog computing resources aiming to minimize processing and networking power consumption. We develop a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model that jointly optimizes access points (APs) selection, wavelength assignment and fog computing resources allocation to serve the requests of users. Compared to a statically preconfigured AP selection and wavelength assignment based on maximizing signal-to-interference-plus-noise ratio (SINR) independently of fog resource placement, the joint optimization achieves lower average total power consumption, driven by a reduction in processing power. These savings are attributed to the ability of the joint optimization to allow AP sharing across users and selecting more energy-efficient wavelengths that still satisfy quality of service (QoS) requirements.

</details>


### [187] [Multi-UAV Tracking Evaluation Using 5G Uplink Signals on an O-RAN ISAC Simulation Testbed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10784)
*Arun K. Gurung,Satha K. Sathananthan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在O-RAN仿真测试平台上（基于OpenAirInterface、FlexRIC和Sionna RT）对多目标检测、关联和跟踪进行端到端评估。该系统将5G NR上行探测参考信号重新用作无源雷达波形，针对反无人机指挥控制（C2）需求进行评估。实验使用三架不同高度、速度和RCS（-8至-20 dBsm）的无人机，在双基地结构和8单元平面接收阵列下，发现一旦所有目标被检测到，限制因素是竞争而非灵敏度——约74%的相干处理间隔中两个目标共享一个最近检测点，且目标被检测的频率远高于被跟踪的频率。阵列仰角虽无法分离共享同一距离-多普勒单元的目标，但在58%的间隔中决定了关联。并发轨迹通过SAPIENT接口从RIC xApp导出到C2融合节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅以检测性能作为评估指标，而非从反无人机C2用户的实际需求角度评估多目标检测、关联与跟踪的端到端性能，缺乏在O-RAN架构下对无源雷达多目标跟踪瓶颈的系统性分析。

Method: 构建基于OpenAirInterface、FlexRIC和Sionna RT的O-RAN仿真测试平台，将5G NR上行探测参考信号重构为无源雷达波形；采用8单元平面接收阵列的双基地配置，评估3架不同属性无人机；通过跟踪-关联机制、仰角辅助关联以及RIC xApp到C2融合节点的SAPIENT接口导出并发轨迹，并结合10个噪声种子进行仿真验证。

Result: 一旦所有目标被检测到，多目标跟踪的瓶颈是竞争而非灵敏度：约74%的相干处理间隔中两个目标共享同一最近检测点；仰角虽无法分离共享距离-多普勒单元的目标，但可在58%的间隔中辅助关联决策；目标检测率远高于跟踪率。

Conclusion: 多目标跟踪性能的关键限制因素是竞争性关联而非检测灵敏度，仰角信息在关联决策中具有重要辅助价值；该方法为仿真-only评估，局限在于单一几何配置，但系统性揭示了影响多目标跟踪性能的关键因素。

Abstract: We evaluate multi-target detection, association and tracking end to end on an O-RAN simulation testbed built from OpenAirInterface, FlexRIC and Sionna RT that repurposes the 5G NR uplink sounding reference signal as a passive radar waveform, and against what a counter-UAS command-and- control (C2) consumer requires rather than by detection alone. Three UAVs differing in altitude, velocity and radar cross section (-8 to -20 dBsm) fly one bistatic pair with an 8-element planar receive array. Once every target is detected the binding limit is contention, not sensitivity - two targets share one nearest detection in about 74% of coherent processing intervals, and targets are detected far more often than they are tracked. Elevation from that array cannot separate targets sharing a range-Doppler cell, but it decides association in 58% of intervals. Concurrent tracks are exported from the RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC) xApp to a C2 fusion node over a SAPIENT interface carrying a calibrated detection confidence, validated at schema level against a mock fusion node. The evaluation is emulation-only on one geometry with 10 noise seeds, the mechanisms are characterized and analyzed to identify the key factors influencing multi-target tracking performance.

</details>


### [188] [A Systematic Sample Size Analysis of ML-Based Path Loss Prediction for LPWAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11083)
*Robert Bitterling,Christian Nettersheim,Jörn Hees,Michael Rademacher*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用机器学习模型（随机森林和k近邻）预测LoRa网络的路径损耗，系统分析了训练集规模对预测精度的影响，并与传统经验模型进行了对比。在城市真实部署数据上，ML模型在训练集内插值场景表现优异（RMSE低于6.5 dB），但留一网关外推测试揭示了其泛化能力的局限性，特别是在未见网关位置时精度会显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRa等低功耗广域网被广泛应用于智慧城市，需要精确的路径损耗预测以进行有效网络规划。然而，传统经验传播模型在这些场景下精度有限，因此需要探索更精确的预测方法。

Method: 采用两种机器学习模型：基于LiDAR地形特征的随机森林（Random Forest）和基于坐标数据的k近邻（k-Nearest Neighbors）。使用城市实际部署的LoRa测量数据，系统分析训练集规模对预测精度的影响，并采用随机池化划分和留一网关（leave-one-gateway-out）两种评估策略。

Result: 在随机池化划分下，两种ML模型在所有训练集规模下均优于基线模型。最大训练规模时，ML模型RMSE低于6.5 dB，而最佳基线为9.7 dB。留一网关测试表明：RF对未见网关有位置依赖的迁移能力（部分网关仅适度退化，部分误差较大），而仅使用坐标的k-NN在网关位置不可见时表现显著退化。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型在LoRa路径损耗预测中优于传统经验模型，可实现高精度的部署内插值；但在跨网关外推场景下存在泛化局限，其中基于地形特征的RF比仅依赖坐标的k-NN具有更好的迁移能力。

Abstract: Low Power Wide Area Networks like LoRa are increasingly deployed for smart city applications, requiring accurate path loss prediction for effective network planning. Traditional (empirical) propagation models often exhibit limited accuracy in these scenarios. We investigate machine learning models for LoRa path loss prediction, systematically analyzing how prediction accuracy scales with training set size using real-world measurements from an urban deployment. Our approach employs a Random Forest with LiDAR-derived terrain features and k-Nearest Neighbors with coordinate data, comparing their performance against established empirical models and specialized LPWAN models. Under random pooled splits, both ML models consistently outperform the considered baseline models across the evaluated training-set sizes. At maximum training size, they achieve RMSE values below 6.5 dB compared to 9.7 dB for the best baseline, indicating accurate within-deployment interpolation. A leave-one-gateway-out check qualifies this result: RF shows placement-dependent transfer to held-out gateways, with moderate degradation for several gateways but larger errors for others, whereas coordinate-only k-NN degrades substantially when the gateway location is unseen

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [N2NMatcher: Towards Inlining-Resilient Binary Decomposition and Module Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10043)
*Ang Jia,He Jiang,Zhipeng Yang,Zhilei Ren,Xiaochen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出N2NMatcher，一个对函数内联具有鲁棒性的二进制分解与模块匹配框架，用于程序级二进制代码相似性分析。它通过分层图神经网络预测稳定的锚点函数，基于锚点进行模块分解，并使用学习到的模块图嵌入进行匹配，显著提升了分解质量和匹配精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序级二进制代码相似性分析方法通常按照函数调用图（FCG）结构分解二进制程序并按包含函数匹配模块，但函数内联会同时改变FCG结构和函数语义，导致现有分解和匹配方法效果下降。

Method: 首先进行实证研究，考察跨编译设置下是否仍存在能提供稳定模块边界的函数。然后提出N2NMatcher框架：(1) 使用分层图神经网络编码由操作码序列、控制流结构和FCG调用上下文构建的ACFG-FCG表示，预测稳定的锚点函数；(2) 基于锚点执行模块边界约束的分解；(3) 使用学习到的模块图嵌入进行模块匹配。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有工作相比，N2NMatcher在分解质量和模块匹配准确率上均有提升，能够实现更有效的程序级二进制代码相似性分析。

Conclusion: N2NMatcher通过识别稳定锚点函数并据此进行分解和匹配，有效解决了函数内联带来的挑战，为程序级BCSA提供了一种更具鲁棒性的解决方案。

Abstract: Program-level Binary Code Similarity Analysis (BCSA) aims to identify semantically similar code regions across binary programs, serving as a fundamental technique for software plagiarism detection, vulnerability search, and malware analysis. Existing approaches often decompose binaries into modules following the structure of function call graphs (FCGs) and then match these modules by their contained functions. However, function inlining changes both FCG structures and binary function semantics, making existing decomposition and module matching methods less effective.
  In this work, we propose N2NMatcher, an inlining-resilient framework for binary decomposition and module matching. We first conduct an empirical study to examine whether binaries still contain functions that provide stable module boundaries across compilation settings. N2NMatcher learns to predict such functions as anchors using a hierarchical graph neural network that encodes binary ACFG-FCG representations built from opcode sequences, control-flow structures, and FCG calling context. It then performs anchor-bounded decomposition and matches the generated modules using learned module graph embeddings. Experimental results show that N2NMatcher improves both the decomposition quality and module matching accuracy compared to existing works, enabling more effective program-level BCSA.

</details>


### [190] [Exploring Semantic Stability Across Reviews in the Linux Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10101)
*Lucas Ciziks,Paulo Meirelles,Marco Aurélio Gerosa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对Linux IIO子系统的10,117个函数轨迹进行逐版本追踪，发现在代码审查中，虽然直接阅读显示函数相似度极高（接近100%），但这主要是由75.3%从未被修改的函数造成的假象。限制在被实际编辑的轨迹后，语义保持度仍很高（均值0.990 vs 基准0.909），但漂移主要集中在第一轮审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的代码审查研究通常只研究最终合并的补丁，而不与首次提交版本比较，导致对代码审查如何改变补丁的认识不完整。作者希望从函数级别追踪代码在审查过程中跨版本的变化轨迹。

Method: 追踪10,117个函数在Linux IIO子系统补丁历史中的轨迹，将每个函数与无关函数对作为基线进行相似度比较。区分从未被文本修改的函数与真正被编辑的函数，并控制组成效应后进行统计分析。

Result: 75.3%的追踪轨迹从未被文本修改，造成相似度虚高。在真正被编辑的轨迹中，语义保持度高（均值0.990 vs 0.909基线），漂移集中在第一轮审查；控制编辑函数数量后存在统计上可检测但很小的残余效应。

Conclusion: 近天花板相似度可能反映语义被保留，也可能反映测量工具无法检测小而局部编辑的重要性。作者将该工作定位为初步探索，并概述了下一步研究方向。

Abstract: Code review is credited with substantially changing a patch's code between its first submission and the version that eventually lands. However, prior work typically studied only the final merged patch without comparing it to the first submission. We present a function-level measurement that tracks 10,117 trajectories (each function followed across the numbered revisions of one patch series) through the patch history of the Linux IIO subsystem, comparing similarity scores against unrelated function pairs as a baseline. A naive reading yields near-total similarity, but this is largely an artifact of composition: 75.3% of tracked trajectories are never textually modified between versions, contributing a trivial 100% similarity that inflates the headline. Restricting to the trajectories with a real edit, semantic purpose is still largely preserved (mean similarity 0.990 vs. a 0.909 baseline), but drift appears to concentrate in the first review round mainly because later rounds contain more functions that nobody touched, not because edits become more conservative over time. After controlling for it, a statistically detectable but small residual effect remains. This points to an open question: whether near-ceiling similarity reflects preserved purpose or a measurement tool that cannot detect the significance of small, localized edits. We present this work as a first look and outline next steps.

</details>


### [191] [One Recipe, Many Harnesses: What Self-Evolution Encodes Across Languages and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10178)
*Siqi Yang,Qianlan Yang,Yu-Xiong Wang,Saurabh Pujar,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过固定演化配方，在8种编程语言和3个基础模型上分析自演化harness（智能体自我改进系统）的演化产物，发现其收益源于对模型可恢复执行缺陷的补偿，并揭示了跨语言共享的抽象剧本与语言特定的生态机制，最终可蒸馏为通用harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究仅报告自演化harness的总体性能提升，但未分析演化产物到底编码了什么——是基准特定适配、语言特定工程知识，还是对底层模型局限的补偿？作者希望解耦这些因素，让演化产物可解释、可归因。

Method: 固定演化配方，在8种编程语言（Multi-SWE-Bench）和3个基础模型的网格上运行演化循环；每条编辑都通过类型化失败信号路由并记录为可证伪契约，使每次修改在演化后可归因，进而系统性地比较各单元的表现与缺陷分布。

Result: (1)演化循环在大多数单元上优于最小种子和人工设计的小型SWE-agent脚手架，但存在两个零效果区域；(2)收益补偿的是可恢复的执行缺陷，缺陷质量接近零时收益也接近零，缺陷主导类型因单元而异；(3)演化harness跨语言共享抽象剧本，但用几乎不相交的语言生态系统机制实例化；(4)共享核心可迁移并蒸馏为通用harness，但生态边际抵抗迁移，需要原生重新演化。

Conclusion: 演化出的harness应被重新理解为一种可读的补偿层，由语言的工程需求与模型的行为差距共同塑造，而非不透明的基准调优脚手架。

Abstract: Self-evolving harnesses are closed-loop systems in which an agent inspects its own rollouts and edits its prompts, tools, and memory. They reliably improve coding agents in evaluations, but prior work reports aggregate gains rather than analyzing what the evolved artifacts encode. It therefore remains unclear whether they encode benchmark-specific adaptations, language-specific engineering knowledge, or compensation for limitations of the underlying model. We disentangle these factors by holding an evolution recipe fixed across a grid of eight programming languages (Multi-SWE-Bench) and three base models, and analyzing the resulting harnesses. The recipe routes every edit through a typed failure signal and records it as a falsifiable contract, making each modification attributable after evolution. Four findings emerge. (1)The loop improves held-out solve rates over both a minimal seed and the manually designed mini-SWE-agent scaffold in most cells, but with two null regions. (2)Gains compensate recoverable execution defects, where defect mass is near zero, and gain is near zero; which defect dominates is cell-specific. A harness closes the gap between what a policy can do and what it does. (3)Evolved harnesses share an abstract playbook across languages but instantiate it with almost disjoint language ecosystem machinery. (4)The shared core transfers and can be distilled into one universal harness, while an ecosystem margin resists both and requires native re-evolution. Together, these results recast the evolved harness as a legible compensation layer, shaped jointly by the language's engineering demands and the model's behavioral gaps, rather than an opaque benchmark-tuned scaffold.

</details>


### [192] [Bias Smells in AI Software Development: Recognizing Potential Sources of Fairness Debt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10248)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Rodrigo Spinola*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过组织内的探索性案例研究，识别出AI软件项目中预示公平性债务的六种"偏差气味"指标，将其作为软件质量保障中可观察的公平性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性债务源于AI开发中的缺陷，可能导致社会危害。尽管AI公平性日益受到关注，但关于从业者在开发过程中如何识别公平性债务潜在来源的证据仍然有限，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对某组织内四个AI项目进行探索性案例研究，通过25名专业人员的半结构化访谈和开放式问卷收集数据，辅以内部沟通渠道和项目文档的观察，采用迭代定性编码、备忘录和持续比较法进行分析。

Result: 识别出六种反复出现的指标，即"偏差气味"：上下文过度简化、数据集不平衡、指标不足、临时性测试、个体多样性意识缺失和同质化团队构成。这些气味涵盖软件开发的技术和人力方面，预示着可能引入或强化偏见并导致公平性债务的条件。

Conclusion: 偏差气味将软件气味范式扩展到公平性领域，通过可观察的指标为将公平性纳入软件质量保障提供了基础。

Abstract: Context: Fairness debt arises from AI development shortcomings that may lead to societal harms. While technical and social debt concern software design decisions and team dynamics, respectively, fairness debt captures the long-term consequences of development decisions that may reinforce bias and inequities in AI-based software systems. Despite growing attention to AI fairness, limited evidence exists on how practitioners recognize potential sources of fairness debt during development. Aim: This study investigates the indicators practitioners recognize as signaling potential sources of fairness debt in AI-based software projects. Method: We conducted an exploratory case study of four AI projects within one organization. Data were collected from 25 professionals through semi-structured interviews and open-ended questionnaires, complemented by observation of internal communication channels and project documentation, and analyzed using iterative qualitative coding, memoing, and constant comparison. Results: We identified six recurring indicators, termed bias smells: Context Oversimplification, Dataset Imbalance, Metrics Inadequacies, Ad hoc Testing, Individual Diversity Unawareness, and Homogeneous Team Composition. These smells span technical and human aspects of software development and signal conditions that may introduce or reinforce bias and contribute to fairness debt. Conclusion: Bias smells extend the software smell paradigm to fairness and provide a foundation for incorporating fairness into software quality assurance through observable indicators.

</details>


### [193] [Enhancing Reliability of Symbolic Execution Tools for Smart Contract Analysis through Rule-Based False Positive Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10265)
*Muhammad Ali Hassan Ahmad,Muhammad Hashim Ali,Muhammad Ali Amer,Muhammad Naiman Jalil,Muhammad Hassan,Affan Rauf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了以太坊智能合约安全分析工具Mythril（基于符号执行）产生大量误报的问题，通过分析误报的根因，设计了基于规则的过滤方法来减少误报，在保持真实漏洞检测能力的同时显著提升了工具的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约安全漏洞检测工具（如Mythril）虽然能发现安全漏洞，但会产生大量误报，导致安全分析人员浪费大量时间和精力去调查虚假警报，从而分散了处理真正漏洞的资源。因此需要评估和改进这些工具的误报率。

Method: 首先分析Mythril生成的漏洞报告中误报的普遍性，探究这些不准确结果的根因；基于获得的洞察设计规则化（rule-based）的方法来减少误报；将规则实现于Mythril中最有影响的漏洞类型上，并评估其有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的规则方法在显著减少误报的同时，没有损害真实漏洞的检测能力，从而增强了工具的可靠性。

Conclusion: 基于根因分析的规则化过滤方法能够有效降低符号执行工具（如Mythril）的误报率，在不牺牲检测能力的前提下提升智能合约安全分析工具的实用性和可信度。

Abstract: A blockchain is a decentralized, secure ledger system that enables transparent and immutable record-keeping, essential for trust and security in digital transactions. Smart contracts are self-executing agreements encoded on a blockchain, enabling different parties to fulfill the terms of the agreement automatically. These contracts trigger corresponding actions when conditions are met, ensuring decentralized and transparent transactions. Writing reliable smart contracts is challenging due to the lack of standardization. To find security vulnerabilities, tools based on various approaches, including symbolic execution, are used. However, these tools often report a large number of false positives, raising concerns about their reliability. The time and effort spent investigating false positives diverts resources from addressing actual vulnerabilities. Therefore, such tools must also be evaluated according to the rate of false positives they exhibit. More importantly, the algorithms and heuristics used by the tools must be enhanced to distinguish between true vulnerabilities and false alarms. In this paper, we first demonstrate the prevalence of false positives in vulnerability reports generated by Mythril, a symbolic execution-based analysis tool for Ethereum smart contracts. We analyze the root causes of these inaccuracies and devise a rule-based approach based on the gained insight to reduce false positives. We implement our rules for the most impactful vulnerabilities in Mythril and assess the effectiveness of our approach. Our results show a significant reduction in false positives without compromising the detection of true vulnerabilities, thus enhancing the tool's reliability.

</details>


### [194] [Comprendia: AI-Augmented Code Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10290)
*Costain Nachuma,Minhaz F. Zibran*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Comprendia 是一个 Eclipse 插件，将结构化依赖关系可视化与 LLM 驱动的代码解释整合在共享交互图上，用于 Java 程序理解。它通过多边依赖图、基于图感知的被调用方剪枝（GACP）的 LLM 解释、克隆检测覆盖层和 CVE 风险覆盖层四大支柱来辅助开发者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升 Java 程序理解效率，将静态依赖图分析与 LLM 自然语言解释相结合，同时保证 LLM 提示的可复现性和可追溯性，让开发者在对图导航时获得上下文相关的代码解释。

Method: 提出四支柱方法：(1) 多边类型依赖图支持实时搜索和多种布局；(2) 引入 Graph-Aware Callee Pruning (GACP) 策略，利用图距离、继承折叠和边类型权重来选择相关被调用方，生成跨 LLM 家族可复现且可追溯到可见图节点的提示；(3) 克隆检测覆盖层突出重复代码并提供提取到父类重构建议；(4) 基于 OSV.dev 的 CVE 风险覆盖层。

Result: 在一个包含已知克隆和漏洞的 Java 项目上进行了演示，展示了统一图基板如何支持代码理解，同时保持开发者对流程的控制。工具提供演示视频链接。

Conclusion: Comprendia 证明将依赖图可视化作为 LLM 提示的"真相来源"可以有效提升程序理解的上下文相关性、可审计性和开发者自主性，为后续类似集成工具提供了设计范式。

Abstract: Comprendia is an Eclipse plugin that integrates structural dependency visualization with LLM-powered code explanation on a shared interactive graph for Java program comprehension. The tool rests on four pillars: (1) a multi-edge-type dependency graph with live search and multiple layouts; (2) LLM explanations grounded in Graph-Aware Callee Pruning (GACP), an auditable strategy that selects relevant callees using the same graph the developer navigates; (3) a clone-detection overlay that highlights duplication and suggests extract-to-parent refactoring opportunities; and (4) a CVE risk overlay powered by OSV.dev. GACP uses graph distance, inheritance collapse, and edge-type weighting to produce prompts that are reproducible across LLM families and traceable to visible graph nodes. We demonstrate Comprendia on a Java project containing known clones and vulnerabilities, showing how the unified graph substrate supports comprehension while keeping the developer in control. Screencast: https://youtu.be/1wlh_RYehzA

</details>


### [195] [Does the way we write a theory change the program an LLM builds from it? A prospective randomized study of renderer format in LLM theory-to-program translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10314)
*Andre Panossian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过前瞻性预注册的随机实验，测试了大语言模型在将理论转化为可执行程序时，不同的呈现格式（结构化干预契约 vs 连接性散文）是否系统性地改变其生成的程序。结果发现两种格式并未产生预期中统一、族不变、可分类的行为几何，主假设均未获支持，同账户可识别性接近随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口头理论无法直接运行，翻译为可执行模型需要对变量、干预和交互做出选择。作者希望检验LLM在进行这种"理论到程序"翻译时，呈现格式（渲染器格式）是否会产生系统性的行为差异，从而划定规范格式效应在LLM理论-程序翻译中的边界。

Method: 采用前瞻性预注册随机实验设计：16个独立分配比特将32对渲染器槽位分配到两种格式（结构化干预契约和连接性散文）；用两个固定的LLM快照翻译5个匿名理论叙述，生成320个预先授权的单次程序；用确定性评估器测量原子有限差分响应（H1）和混合交互响应（H2）；通过精确随机化推断和27个预注册支持标准，在符号线性和幅度秩两条管道中进行评估。

Result: H1和H2均返回登记裁决NOT_SUPPORTED；108项标准评估中仅19项通过。同账户可识别性接近随机（AUC 0.469-0.523，而注册阈值为0.80）。一个H2匹配距离端点在符号线性管道中通过并经受住多重性校正，但相应的幅度秩结果未达到注册效应量下限，因此不满足联合支持规则。

Conclusion: 渲染器格式并未产生预测中统一、族不变、可分类的行为几何。该结果对LLM理论到程序翻译中的规范格式效应划定了一个具体边界，并提供了完全可审计的随机设计、数据集和软件，用于研究可执行形式化。

Abstract: A verbal theory does not run: translating it into an executable model requires choices about variables, interventions, and interactions. We tested whether the presentation of otherwise identical theoretical content systematically changes the programs produced by large language models. In a prospective preregistered randomized experiment, 16 independent assignment bits allocated 32 paired renderer slots between a structured intervention contract and connected prose. Two pinned LLM snapshots translated five anonymous theoretical accounts, yielding 320 preauthorized single-shot programs in a frozen sparse quadratic language. A deterministic evaluator measured atomic finite-difference responses (H1) and mixed interaction responses (H2). Two primary endpoints assessed cross-model matched-distance reduction and closed-set same-account identifiability, with exact randomization inference and 27 preregistered support criteria evaluated across signed-linear and magnitude-rank pipelines. Both H1 and H2 returned the registered verdict NOT_SUPPORTED; only 19 of 108 criterion evaluations passed. Same-account identifiability remained near chance (AUC 0.469-0.523, against a registered 0.80 threshold). One H2 matched-distance endpoint moved and survived multiplicity correction in the signed-linear pipeline, but the corresponding magnitude-rank result missed the registered effect-size floor, so it did not satisfy the joint support rule. Thus renderer format did not produce the uniform, family-invariant, classifiable behavioral geometry predicted in advance. The result places a concrete boundary on specification-format effects in LLM theory-to-program translation and provides a fully auditable randomized design, datasets, and software for studying executable formalization.

</details>


### [196] [Do Personalized Skills Help Coding Agents? An Empirical Study of Developer Interaction Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10319)
*Shuyan Huang,Kai Du,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从开发者与LLM智能体交互轨迹中提取可复用开发者偏好的框架，通过规则引导的引导生成和基于证据的细化来创建个性化技能，并使用可复现的重放框架进行评估。实验基于206个真实会话和13名开发者，发现个性化技能相比无技能基线仅带来微小且不一致的改进，而跨开发者聚合的通用技能效果最好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体从代码补全工具演变为复杂软件工程任务的解决者，开发者在反复交互中会形成个人偏好。捕获和重用这些偏好可以减少重复纠正并改善人机协作。然而现有工作主要关注任务特定技能，不清楚从交互历史中提取的开发者特定技能是否能泛化到未来任务。

Method: 提出一个从交互轨迹中提取可复用开发者偏好的框架：1) 通过规则引导的引导（rule-based bootstrapping）和证据支撑的细化（evidence-grounded refinement）生成个性化技能；2) 使用可复现的重放框架，配合交互式、轨迹条件化的LLM人类开发者模拟器进行评估。

Result: 在13名开发者的206个真实会话上进行实验，对比无技能、通用技能和其他用户技能基线。发现个性化技能相对于无技能基线仅有微小且不一致的改进，而跨开发者聚合的通用技能获得最大且最一致的增益。进一步分析表明，当开发者偏好频繁出现且历史中包含多个与未来任务相关的示例时，个性化技能更有效。

Conclusion: 研究发现开发者特定个性化只在偏好频繁出现时有效，而广泛可迁移的程序性知识比开发者特定偏好信号更稳健。这些发现为开发者特定个性化何时有效提供了实证见解。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered agents have rapidly evolved from code-completion tools into solvers of complex software engineering tasks. As developers collaborate with coding agents over time, their preferences emerge through repeated interactions and can be used to adapt agent behavior to better meet individual developers' needs. Capturing and reusing these preferences may reduce repeated corrections and improve developer-agent collaboration. Agent skills provide a lightweight mechanism for transferring experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing work primarily focuses on task-specific skills, and it remains unclear whether developer-specific skills distilled from interaction histories can generalize to future tasks. We propose a framework for extracting reusable developer preferences from interaction traces. It first generates personalized skills through rule-based bootstrapping and evidence-grounded refinement, and then evaluates them using a reproducible replay framework with an interactive, trajectory-conditioned LLM-based human developer simulator. We conduct an experiment on 206 real-world developer-agent sessions from 13 developers and compare personalized skills against no-skill, generic-skill, and other-user-skill baselines. Personalized skills provide small and inconsistent improvements over the no-skill baseline, whereas generic skills pooled across developers achieve the largest and most consistent gains. Further analysis suggests that personalized skills become more effective when developer preferences appear frequently, particularly when their histories contain multiple examples relevant to future tasks. These findings provide empirical insights into when developer-specific personalization is effective and demonstrate that broadly transferable procedural knowledge can be more robust than developer-specific preference signals.

</details>


### [197] [Persistent Recursive Worlds Enable Autonomous Software Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10450)
*Beichen Huang,Zhenyu Liang,Bowen Zheng,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoX Genesis框架，将软件项目视为持久化的递归世界，而非依赖持久化的智能体。有限寿命的智能体提出局部变更，递归委派跨路径推进工作，只有被接受的变更才能推进持久版本历史。实验证明该方法可构建完整的Rust C编译器（约25万行，花费仅44美元），支持智能体反复更换后的持续开发，并成功将13个MESA模块重写为Rust实现并获得1.55-6.87倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂软件系统的开发周期远超任何单个编码智能体的生命周期。现有的智能体系统通过持久会话、记忆或共享上下文来保持连续性，但作者认为应让软件项目本身保持持久，而让局部智能体保持有限寿命，从而解决长期软件开发中的连续性挑战。

Method: Genesis将软件表示为持久的递归世界：每个局部世界由接受版本和仓库路径定位；有限寿命的智能体提出局部变更；递归委派机制跨路径转移工作；只有被接受的后果才能推进持久版本历史。系统在形成、延续和再开发三个阶段进行评估。

Result: 使用DeepSeek V4 Flash从零构建了约25万行的Rust C编译器，历时120小时，归档超过1000个智能体片段，仅花费44美元token费用，通过完整c-testsuite及大多数LLVM和Csmith测试。在GLM 5.2生成的编译器世界中，反复更换智能体后仍保持完整测试性能。将13个MESA模块（超10万行Fortran）重写为近9万行Rust，六个数值工作负载中实现1.55-6.87倍中位数加速。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，长期限的软件开发可以围绕持久项目而非持久智能体来组织，这为构建超越单个智能体生命周期的自主软件系统提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Complex software systems develop over timescales that exceed the lifespan of any individual coding agent. Most agentic software systems preserve continuity through persistent sessions, memories, managers or shared context. We introduce EvoX Genesis (hereafter, Genesis), which instead makes the software project persistent while allowing local agents to remain finite-lived. Genesis represents software as a persistent recursive world: each local world is situated by an accepted version and a repository path, finite-lived agents propose local changes, recursive delegation moves work across paths, and only accepted consequences advance the persistent version history. We evaluate this organization across formation, continuation and redevelopment. Starting from a repository with no compiler implementation, Genesis used DeepSeek V4 Flash to build a Rust-based C compiler with about 250k tracked lines; the run lasted over 120 hours, archived over 1,000 agent episodes and incurred only US$44 in model-token charges. The compiler passed the complete c-testsuite and most LLVM and Csmith tests. In a separate compiler world generated with GLM 5.2, development continued after repeated agent replacement while retaining full test performance. Genesis also reimplemented 13 MESA modules with over 100k Fortran lines as a Rust workspace with nearly 90k Rust lines; across six numerical workloads, it achieved median speedups of 1.55--6.87x. These results show that long-horizon software development can be organized around a persistent project rather than a persistent agent.

</details>


### [198] [CausalRepair: Bridging the Causality Gap in Large Language Model-Based Automated Program Repair via Dual-Slicing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10613)
*Linhao Wu,Yizhou Chen,Zhen Yang,Pengyu Xue,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CausalRepair，一种基于最小因果上下文的对话式自动程序修复框架。它通过双切片策略（上下文感知静态切片+执行轨迹动态切片）构建紧凑且因果相关的上下文，引导LLM进行迭代修复，在Defects4J基准上修复313个bug，平均修复成本仅$0.029。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based APR方法存在"因果鸿沟"问题：测试上下文可能嘈杂或不完整，而基于静态分析得到的源码上下文常包含无关且未执行的代码，会误导LLM偏离真正的根因定位。

Method: 采用双切片策略：1) 上下文感知的静态切片用于净化测试语义；2) 基于执行轨迹的动态切片捕获源码中精确的运行时依赖。两者结合构建紧凑、因果相关的上下文，驱动对话式迭代修复。

Result: 在Defects4J V1.2、V2.0和Defects4J-Trans上使用DeepSeek-V3评估，正确修复313个bug，超越ReinFix和TSAPR等SOTA方法，并将平均修复成本降至每bug $0.029。

Conclusion: CausalRepair通过提供最小因果上下文有效解决了LLM修复中的因果鸿沟问题，在修复效果（修复数量）和成本效益（极低修复成本）两方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Automated Program Repair (APR) has recently benefited from Large Language Models (LLMs), yet their effectiveness heavily depends on repair context. Existing LLM-based APR methods suffer from a causality gap: test contexts can be noisy or incomplete, while source contexts derived from static analysis often contain irrelevant and unexecuted code, misleading LLMs from identifying the true root cause. To address this issue, we propose CausalRepair, a conversation-driven APR framework based on minimal causal context, i.e., the essential dependencies required to explain a failure. CausalRepair employs a dual-slicing strategy: context-aware static slicing purifies test semantics, while execution-trace-based dynamic slicing captures precise runtime dependencies in source code. Together, they construct compact, causally relevant contexts to guide iterative repair. We evaluate CausalRepair on Defects4J V1.2, V2.0, and Defects4J-Trans using DeepSeek-V3. CausalRepair correctly fixes 313 bugs on Defects4J, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches such as ReinFix and TSAPR, while reducing the average repair cost to $0.029 per bug.

</details>


### [199] [A Study of Cursorrules Files in GitHub Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10622)
*Shuang Sun,Jafar Akhoundali,Arina Kudriavtseva,Sengim Karayalcin,Olga Gadyatskaya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Cursor AI编程编辑器中的配置文件提示词（.cursorrules文件）进行了首次大规模实证研究，分析了来自11,427个GitHub仓库的12,110个配置文件，揭示了其在2024年中期快速出现、主要集中于小型低活跃度仓库、内容以代码质量和工程实践为主导等特征，并建立了包含65个代码的编码手册。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编程日益普及，提示词成为与AI代理沟通的主要机制。然而，现有研究主要关注对话式提示词，对静态配置文件提示词（prompt files）缺乏系统性研究，存在明显的研究空白。

Method: 收集并分析12,110个.cursorrules文件（来自11,427个GitHub仓库），进行分布、演进和维护特征分析；同时对65个提示词文件进行定性分析，开发了包含65个代码的编码手册，分类开发者如何表达编程意图、项目上下文、工程实践和安全考虑。

Result: 研究发现：.cursorrules文件从2024年中期开始迅速出现；采用集中于小型、低活跃度、单人维护的仓库，表明更多是玩具项目而非专业开发；内容以代码质量和工程实践、项目结构和配置、可维护性为主，安全相关内容较少；.cursorrules与现行的.mdc文件在主题连续性上保持一致。

Conclusion: 配置文件提示词在AI编程中已形成独特生态，但其当前使用模式更多出现在非专业场景。研究揭示了配置文件内容的主题分布和演变趋势，为未来提示词工程研究和工具设计提供了基础。

Abstract: Prompts are the primary mechanism for communicating with AI agents, and they directly influence the quality and reliability of AI-generated code. As AI-assisted programming becomes widely adopted, modern tools increasingly combine dynamic conversational prompts with static configuration-like prompt files. Despite the growing focus on prompt engineering, prior research has primarily focused on conversational prompts, while prompt files remain understudied.
  To address this gap, we conduct an empirical study of configuration prompt files in Cursor, a widely used AI-assisted code editor. We collect and analyze over 12,110 .cursorrules files from 11,427 GitHub repositories to characterize their distribution, evolution, and maintenance. Complementing this, we perform qualitative analysis on a random sample of 65 prompt files and develop a 65-code codebook capturing how developers express programming intent, project context, engineering practices, and security considerations.
  Our results show that .cursorrules files emerged rapidly from mid-2024. Their adoption is concentrated in small-scale, low-activity, single-maintainer repositories, suggesting toy projects rather than professional development. The content of prompt files is dominated by guidance on code quality and engineering practices, project structure and configuration, and maintainability, while security-related content appears less frequently. Our analysis shows that there is a continuity of themes and topics between the now-legacy .cursorrules files and the current standard .mdc files.

</details>


### [200] [LLM Ensemble Fault Classification for Automotive HiL Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10710)
*Hamza Ouarrad,Mohammad Abboush,Andreas Rausch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的多LLM集成框架，用于汽车硬件在环（HiL）验证中的传感器级故障分类。该框架使用故障注入记录的紧凑证据表示，组合多个异构大语言模型的输出，通过置信度加权投票提升诊断鲁棒性、排序质量、置信度可靠性和可解释性。在汽油发动机和电动汽车HiL系统上评估，Top-3集成（Mistral Small 24B、Qwen2.5 32B、Phi-4 14B）达到0.917 Top-1准确率和0.902 MCC，优于任何单个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车HiL验证产生大量多变量测试记录，但现有分析方法面临人工审查工作量大、基于规则的方法局限性高以及缺乏可解释诊断决策等问题。虽然机器学习/深度学习方法改善了故障诊断，但往往需要大规模标注数据集、跨操作条件泛化能力差，且对预测结果提供的解释有限。因此需要一种既能提升诊断性能又能提供可解释性的方法。

Method: 提出一个解释性多LLM集成框架：(1)使用紧凑证据表示（compact evidence representations）对故障注入记录进行特征编码；(2)组合多个异构大语言模型（Mistral Small 24B、Qwen2.5 32B、Phi-4 14B等）的输出；(3)采用置信度加权投票（confidence-weighted voting）进行集成决策；(4)在汽油发动机和电动汽车HiL系统上，覆盖三种驾驶设置和十种单故障类别进行评估。

Result: 单个模型中Mistral Small 24B表现最佳（Top-1准确率0.903，MCC 0.887，Brier分数0.102）。Top-3集成进一步将场景平均结果提升至Top-1准确率0.917、宏F1 0.913、MCC 0.902，且在校准度上优于其他测试的集成策略。Top-5集成未能超越Top-3配置，表明模型互补性比集成规模更重要。

Conclusion: 协调的多LLM推理能够支持鲁棒、校准良好且工程师可解释的汽车HiL验证故障分类。模型互补性比集成规模更关键，该框架为汽车验证中的智能故障诊断提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Automotive HiL validation generates large multivariate test recordings whose analysis remains challenging due to manual review effort, rule-based limitations, and the need for explainable diagnostic decisions. Recent machine-learning and deep-learning approaches have improved fault diagnosis, but they often require large labelled datasets, generalise poorly across operating conditions, and provide limited insight into their predictions. This paper proposes an explainable multi-LLM ensemble framework for sensor-level fault classification in automotive validation. The framework uses compact evidence representations of fault-injection recordings and combines the outputs of heterogeneous large language models to improve diagnostic robustness, ranking quality, confidence reliability, and interpretability. The approach is evaluated on gasoline-engine and electric-vehicle HiL systems across three driving settings and ten single-fault classes. Among the individual models, Mistral Small~24B provides the strongest overall single-model trade-off, achieving 0.903 Top-1 accuracy, 0.887 MCC, and the lowest Brier score of 0.102. The final Top-3 ensemble combines Mistral Small~24B, Qwen2.5~32B, and Phi-4~14B using confidence-weighted voting, improving the scenario-averaged results to 0.917 Top-1 accuracy, 0.913 macro F1, and 0.902 MCC, while also providing the best calibration among the tested ensemble strategies. A Top-5 ensemble does not improve over the Top-3 configuration, indicating that model complementarity is more important than ensemble size. The results show that coordinated multi-LLM reasoning can support robust, calibrated, and engineer-interpretable fault classification for automotive HiL validation.

</details>


### [201] [Conflict Extraction in Probabilistic Datalog Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10755)
*Siyu Chen,Chungha Sung,Xuyang Li,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PPProbe，一个专门针对概率Datalog分析中的冲突提取器，通过利用Datalog推导图的结构来高效枚举最小不可满足子集（MUSes），解决概率分析输出中"单独可信但相互不一致"的告警问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率Datalog扩展（用于指针分析、数据竞争检测、侧信道分析等）虽然能对告警按可能性排序，但边际概率无法保证联合可满足性，导致输出中可能出现互斥的告警组合，浪费开发者的调查精力。这是确定性分析中不存在的新挑战。

Method: PPProbe将不一致告警形式化为MUSes，利用Datalog推导图的结构引导搜索朝向可能的冲突，并通过自底向上的UNSAT推理来剪枝搜索空间，而非泛化地改进MUS枚举算法。

Result: 在70个基准测试（涵盖功率侧信道分析、数据竞争检测、语义差异分析和贝叶斯网络推理）上，PPProbe比最先进的MUS枚举器吞吐量高2.5到24倍；其识别出的冲突可保守估计减少平均47.7%的互不一致告警。

Conclusion: 通过利用Datalog推导图的领域特定结构，可以有效解决概率分析中告警互不一致的问题，显著提升冲突检测效率并减少开发者的无效调查工作。

Abstract: Probabilistic extensions of Datalog enable static analyses such as pointer analysis, data race detection, and side-channel analysis to rank alarms by likelihood, but this added expressiveness also introduces a new challenge absent from deterministic analyses: the final output may contain alarms that are individually plausible yet mutually inconsistent, because marginal probabilities do not guarantee joint satisfiability. As a result, developers may spend effort investigating combinations of alarms that can never co-occur in any possible world. We address this problem by formalizing such inconsistencies as minimal unsatisfiable subsets (MUSes) and introducing PPProbe, a conflict extractor specialized for probabilistic Datalog analyses. Rather than improving MUS enumeration in general, PPProbe exploits the structure of Datalog derivation graphs to guide the search toward likely conflicts and prune the search space through bottom-up UNSAT inference. We evaluate PPProbe on 70 benchmarks from power side-channel analysis, data race detection, semantic diffing, and Bayesian-network inference. The results show that PPProbe achieves 2.5 to 24 times higher throughput than state-of-the-art MUS enumerators, and that the conflicts it identifies yield a conservative estimate of false-positive reduction, filtering out an average of 47.7% of mutually inconsistent alarms.

</details>


### [202] [Extending Triple Graph Grammars to Formalize Complex View Definitions on Families of Models - Long Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10785)
*Lars König,Jens Kosiol*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文继续为NeoJoin视图定义语言配备基于三元图文法（TGGs）的形式化语义，通过引入skip语义来填补TGG在支持重叠查询翻译方面的空白，从而实现模型与视图之间的自动化、增量同步，并具备高形式化保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于视图的开发是管理复杂系统和系统之系统开发复杂性的重要技术，但视图定义语言需要同时满足两个要求：易于使用且通用（供不同背景开发者使用），以及具有精确的形式化语义（以便以受控方式集成各个视图的工作）。

Method: 在之前工作的基础上，为NeoJoin视图定义语言配备基于三元图文法（TGGs）的形式化语义。针对TGG在翻译重叠查询方面的不足，引入skip语义，即允许TGG规则根据应用上下文跳过规则规定的某些动作。

Result: 该方法为获得模型与视图之间的自动化、增量同步过程铺平道路，这些过程具有高形式化保证；同时作为TGG表达性和可用性的进一步案例研究。

Conclusion: 本文识别并解决了TGG在支持视图定义语言中重叠查询翻译方面的一个空白，通过skip语义增强TGG规则的能力，既推进了NeoJoin的形式化工作，也丰富了TGG的理论与应用。

Abstract: View-based development is an important technique to manage complexity and facilitate cooperation in the development of modern and complex systems of systems. This approach places high demands on view definition languages used in the development process. First, such a language needs to be approachable and extremely versatile so that developers from diverse backgrounds are able to use it and can define views for a diverse set of different tasks. Yet, the language also needs to have a precise and formal semantics so that it is possible to integrate the work performed on various views in a controlled manner to obtain a coherent overall system. In this paper, we continue previous work of equipping the NeoJoin view definition language with a formal semantics that is based on triple graph grammars (TGGs). This paves the way for obtaining automated and incremental synchronization procedures between models and views that come with high formal guarantees for their behavior. Simultaneously, our work serves as a further case study of the expressivity and usability of TGGs. We identify one gap, namely convenient support for the translation of overlapping queries from the view definition language, and tackle that gap by introducing a skip semantics for TGG rules, i.e., allowing to skip certain actions a rule prescribes, depending on the application context.

</details>


### [203] [From Pattern Detection to Composition Analysis in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10882)
*Neilson Carlos Leite Ramalho,Erico Augusto da Silva,Anthony Accioly,Higor Amario de Souza,Marcos Lordello Chaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文扩展了量子软件模式挖掘工具，新增两个匹配通道和词汇扩展步骤，在未见于知识库的Qrisp框架上达到0.712的micro-F1（对比无扩展的0.449），并构建组合图分析模式在框架内的组装方式、共现原因及检测来源，同时发布了开源挖掘流水线、知识库（含286个框架组件映射）和模式使用数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件模式虽有较完善的理论分类（61种模式目录），但缺乏实证证据表明它们在实际开源项目中如何被采用。作者先前开发的挖掘工具仅覆盖了已有知识库中的框架，面对新框架时准确性有限，且对模式如何组合、为何共现等深层问题缺乏分析。

Method: 在已有挖掘工具基础上：(1)增加两个额外的匹配通道；(2)引入词汇扩展步骤提升跨框架匹配能力；(3)构建组合图（composition graphs），记录与模式关联的高层框架组件之间的调用关系并存入图数据库；(4)利用LLM集成对新增组件进行分类以维护知识库；(5)在Qrisp（不在知识库中的框架）上进行定量评估。

Result: 在Qrisp框架上micro-F1达0.712，显著高于无词汇扩展的0.449。通过组合图分析了模式实现的组装方式、模式共现原因，以及模式检测计数中哪些来自开发者直接调用的组件、哪些来自框架内部调用引入。发布了qpa开源流水线、包含286个组件映射的知识库及模式使用数据集。

Conclusion: 词汇扩展和额外匹配通道显著提升了模式挖掘的跨框架准确性，组合图分析揭示了模式在真实框架中的组装和共现规律，开源工具链和数据集为量子模式采纳与演化的可复现研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Quantum software patterns provide high-level abstractions for building quantum programs, but there is still little empirical evidence on how they are adopted in practice. In prior work, we extended an existing quantum-pattern atlas into a 61-pattern catalog, created a knowledge base that links framework components to those patterns, and built a tool that mines pattern implementations from open-source code. We applied this tool on 80 projects and find that all 23 patterns occur in practice. In this work, we extend the tool with two additional matching channels and a vocabulary expansion step, and execute a quantitative evaluation of its accuracy on Qrisp, a framework not present in the knowledge base, reaching a micro-F1 of 0.712 against 0.449 without the expansion step. We then construct composition graphs that record calls among the high-level framework components associated with patterns and store them in a graph database. We use these graphs to examine how pattern implementations are assembled inside each framework, why patterns co-occur, and how much of a pattern's detection count comes from components called directly by developers rather than introduced through internal framework calls. We release qpa, an open-source mining pipeline, together with the knowledge base, which maps 286 framework components across five sources to the pattern catalog, maintained with the support of an LLM ensemble that classifies newly extracted components, and the resulting pattern usage dataset, to support reproducible studies on the adoption and evolution of quantum patterns.

</details>


### [204] [GitSkills: A Dataset of Agent Skills on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10906)
*Giuseppe Destefanis,Daniel Graziotin,Matteo Vaccargiu,Marco Ortu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GitSkills数据集，包含2026年7月从282,200个公共GitHub仓库收集的3,797,117个SKILL.md文件，以自包含SQLite文件形式保存，用于研究agent技能的采用、复用、结构、作者归属、维护和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent技能（包含SKILL.md文件的文件夹）是一种新兴的人工产物，与SE研究社区通常挖掘的对象不同：它们主要用自然语言编写、由模型在运行时概率性选择、没有编译器或类型检查器验证，且没有中央注册表。开发者如何编写、复用和维护技能是一个实证问题，但现有数据集并未记录这一群体。

Method: 从282,200个公共GitHub仓库收集SKILL.md文件，保留每个文件出现位置（仓库、路径、内容哈希），将相同文件分组为内容组，为每组一个代表文件富化完整文本、解析的front matter、文件夹内容、仓库元数据以及部分文件的提交历史，最终打包为单个自包含SQLite文件。

Result: 构建了包含3,797,117个SKILL.md文件、1,877,981个不同内容的数据集GitSkills，来源覆盖282,200个公共仓库。

Conclusion: GitSkills数据集为研究agent技能的实际使用情况提供了基础，支持对技能的采用、复用、结构、作者归属、维护和安全性等多维度的实证研究。

Abstract: An agent skill is a folder containing a SKILL.md file with instructions for a language-model agent, optionally accompanied by scripts and reference files. The agent loads the skill when it judges that a task matches the skill description. Anthropic introduced the format in October 2025 as an open specification. Nine months later, we find that skill files in the millions sit in public GitHub repositories. Skills are unlike the artifacts the SE research community usually mines: they are written mainly in natural language, a model selects them probabilistically at run time, and no compiler or type checker verifies the selection. They also have no central registry or package manager, so they spread by copying folders between repositories. How developers write, reuse, and maintain skills is therefore an empirical question, and no existing dataset records this population. We present GitSkills, a dataset of 3,797,117 SKILL.md files collected from 282,200 public repositories in July 2026. The dataset retains every file occurrence with its repository, path, and content hash. It groups identical files into 1,877,981 distinct contents and enriches one representative per group with the full text, parsed front matter, folder contents, repository metadata, and, for a subset, the commit history of the file. A single self- contained SQLite file supports research on the adoption, reuse, structure, authorship, maintenance, and security of agent skills.

</details>


### [205] [Understanding the Architecture of Coding Agents: An Exploratory Study Using a Research Prototype](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10934)
*Marco Tulio Valente*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对编码代理（coding agents）缺乏系统性架构描述的问题，提出了Ark——一个极简开源编码代理，用于研究和教育，并配套ArkBench轻量级基准测试（含10个软件维护任务）。使用gpt-5.4-mini时，Ark解决了8/10的任务且token消耗适中。文章还通过最新架构分类法将Ark与SOTA编码代理进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理已成为AI辅助软件开发的主要接口，但其内部架构鲜为人知，缺乏类似于编译器或操作系统那样的系统性架构描述，这阻碍了教学、研究和实验。

Method: 1) 记录编码代理的主要架构组件、职责、交互和执行流程；2) 构建Ark（Agent Research Kit）——保留现代编码代理核心架构机制但强调简洁性的极简开源代理；3) 引入ArkBench——包含10个代表性软件维护与演化任务的轻量级基准；4) 使用近期提出的架构分类法将Ark与SOTA编码代理进行架构对比。

Result: 使用gpt-5.4-mini，Ark成功解决了ArkBench中10个任务中的8个，且token消耗适中。

Conclusion: Ark和ArkBench为编码代理的教学、研究和实验提供了实用基础，填补了编码代理架构系统性描述的空白。

Abstract: Coding agents have rapidly emerged as the primary interface for AI-assisted software development. However, despite their growing adoption, relatively little is known about their internal architecture, and no systematic architectural description comparable to those available for compilers or operating systems currently exists. This paper addresses this gap by documenting the main architectural components of coding agents, explaining their responsibilities, interactions, and execution flow. To support this effort, we also present Ark (Agent Research Kit), a minimal open-source coding agent designed for research and education that preserves the essential architectural mechanisms of modern coding agents while emphasizing simplicity and clarity. We also introduce ArkBench, a lightweight benchmark comprising ten representative software maintenance and evolution tasks. Using gpt-5.4-mini, Ark successfully solved 8 of the 10 tasks while requiring modest token consumption. Finally, we compare the architecture of Ark with those of state-of-the-art coding agents using a recently proposed architectural taxonomy. We hope that both Ark and ArkBench provide a practical foundation for teaching, research, and experimentation on coding agents.

</details>


### [206] [Agentic Configuration Management (ACM): A Reference Configuration Model for Governed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11166)
*Audrey Quessada-Vial*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Configuration Management (ACM)，一个框架无关的配置治理参考模型，用于对异构智能体系统进行统一、版本化的配置管理和治理，并提供Python参考实现及对LangGraph、CrewAI、OpenAI Agents SDK的适配器支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMOps和AgentOps平台支持编排和可观测性，但缺乏统一的配置治理模型，无法将异构智能体系统表示为一致、版本化的配置，导致配置在框架和运行时环境间演化时难以治理和追踪。

Method: ACM提出类型化且独立版本化的Agentic Configuration Items、不可变修订与基线、明确的配置-运行时分离、生命周期与保障语义、依赖感知的变更影响传播以及运行时溯源。异构原生配置通过语义投影归一化为规范配置图（Configuration Graph），在其上执行统一的治理语义。影响语义形式化为有限格上的单调传播，确保收敛性、终止性和最小不动点的唯一性。

Result: 提供了Python参考实现及LangGraph、CrewAI、OpenAI Agents SDK三个框架的适配器。评估包含27个治理场景和9个定量影响传播案例，三个框架在投影后产生治理等价的ACM表示和可复现的治理结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明通用治理语义能够在评估范围内支持跨异构智能体执行抽象的可复现性、可审计性、依赖分析和互操作性。

Abstract: Agentic systems are increasingly composed of heterogeneous agents, prompts, tools, models, skills, composite subsystems, policies, and execution workflows whose configurations evolve across frameworks and runtime environments. Existing LLMOps and AgentOps platforms support orchestration and observability but do not provide a common configuration-governance model for representing and governing these systems as coherent, versioned configurations.
  This paper introduces Agentic Configuration Management (ACM), a framework-independent governance and configuration reference model for heterogeneous agentic systems. ACM combines typed and independently versioned Agentic Configuration Items, immutable revisions and baselines, explicit configuration-runtime separation, lifecycle and assurance semantics, dependency-aware impact propagation, and runtime provenance. Heterogeneous native configurations are normalized through semantic projection into a canonical Configuration Graph on which common governance semantics operate.
  We provide a Python reference implementation with adapters for LangGraph, CrewAI, and the OpenAI Agents SDK. The evaluation combines 27 governance scenarios with nine quantitative impact-propagation cases. For the evaluated configurations, the three frameworks yield governance-equivalent ACM representations and reproducible governance outcomes after projection. The impact semantics are formalized as monotone propagation over a finite lattice, establishing convergence, termination, and uniqueness of the least fixed point above the initial impact valuation. These results provide evidence that common governance semantics can support reproducibility, auditability, dependency analysis, and interoperability across heterogeneous agentic execution abstractions within the evaluated scope.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [Closed-Loop LLM Co-Pilots for Digital Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09949)
*Serge Kernbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大语言模型（LLM）在复杂生物系统中的应用，将其从数据分析工具发展为自主、AI驱动的实验代理。基于49通道植物传感器网络，系统实现了从人在环分析到全自主控制的转变，通过闭环AI-生物界面驱动硬件执行优化微气候、表型分析和压力诱导等操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生物系统研究依赖人类专家进行数据分析和实验设计，面临计算资源和专家劳动力成本高、可及性差等限制。需要将LLM从被动分析工具转变为主动的自主实验代理，以降低数字农业的成本和价值比，使复杂生物系统探索对专家和非专家都更加可及。

Method: 构建了基于49通道植物传感器网络（多光谱、电化学、介电模态）的框架，LLM处理生物物理数据并评估植物生理状态，进而触发硬件执行器以优化微气候、执行表型方案或诱导受控压力情境。该闭环架构建立了直接的AI-生物界面，并在三个案例研究中得到验证，包括垂直农场和单株植物装置。

Result: 在三个案例研究中：1) 最小时间模式将生产周期缩短35%；2) 能量优化模式通过光脉冲利用生理惯性，能耗降低18%仅边际延长栽培时间；3) 自主开发了暗诱导叶绿素积累的意外策略，实现67.9%的节能。此外，在量产规模部署中实现了多参数优化，平衡生物量积累、叶绿素含量和能耗。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM转变为数字农业的自主副驾驶，显著改善成本-价值比，降低计算和专家劳动力约束，为数据驱动的复杂生物系统和生态探索开辟了新途径。

Abstract: This study evaluates the application of Large Language Models (LLMs) in complex biological systems, evolving from data analysis to autonomous, AI-guided experimentation. The framework is driven by data from a 49-channel phytosensor network, encompassing multispectral, electrochemical, and dielectric modalities. To enhance accessibility, the system provides real-time natural-language interpretation for both specialists and non-experts. However, its core advantage lies in the transition from human-in-the-loop analysis to autonomous control. Processing biophysical data, the LLM evaluates plant physiology and triggers hardware actuators to optimize microclimates, execute phenotyping protocols, or induce controlled stress scenarios. This closed-loop architecture establishes a direct AI-biology interface, enabling data-driven exploration of complex biosystems and ecologies. The framework was validated across three case studies, based on a vertical farm and a single-plant setup and deciphered complex micro- and macro-fluctuations in plant physiology. Agents in a production-scale deployment executed multi-parameter optimization, balancing biomass accumulation, chlorophyll content, and energy consumption.
