Motivation: 一阶公式概念综合的搜索空间巨大，LLM 输出往往语义上有希望但并非完全正确，需要一种将 LLM 生成与精确符号验证相结合的方法来提升问题的可解性。

Method: 提出 Hypothesis Frontier 框架：(1) 对每个 LLM 候选公式在所有训练对象上进行精确验证评估；(2) 跨轮次保留最强的已验证假设；(3) 利用剩余错误引导后续生成；(4) 通过符号处理修复无效公式，同时锚定在 LLM 生成的假设上；(5) 简化训练-验证公式而不改变任何训练预测。

Result: 在匹配的模型、问题集和 LLM 轮次预算条件下，Hypothesis Frontier 相比重复原始提示生成解决了显著更多的问题。最终公式选定后，精确简化在许多情况下缩短了训练-验证公式，同时保持所有训练预测不变。

Conclusion: 精确符号推理既能帮助解决更多归纳问题，也能压缩许多最终生成的公式，证明了神经符号结合在概念综合任务中的有效性。

Abstract: First-order concept synthesis asks a system to infer one formula that classifies labeled objects consistently across several finite relational structures. Every candidate can be evaluated exactly, but quantified first-order formulas form a vast search space, and LLM outputs are often semantically promising without being fully correct. We introduce Hypothesis Frontier, a verifier-guided neurosymbolic framework that evaluates each LLM formula on every training object, retains the strongest verified hypothesis across rounds, and uses its remaining errors to guide subsequent generation. Symbolic processing repairs invalid formulas while remaining anchored to the LLM-generated hypothesis, and simplifies train-valid formulas without changing any training prediction. Under matched models, problem sets, and LLM-round budgets, Hypothesis Frontier solves substantially more problems than repeated original-prompt generation. After the final formulas are selected, exact simplification shortens many train-valid formulas while preserving every training prediction. Exact symbolic reasoning therefore helps both to solve more induction problems and to compress many of the resulting formulas.

</details>


### [275] [Enhanced Filtering Algorithms for the Euclidean Traveling Salesperson Problem and its variants in Constraint Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10881)
*Alessandro Bertagnon,Marco Gavanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用几何信息的新型过滤算法，在约束逻辑编程（CLP）框架下解决欧几里得旅行商问题（TSP）及其广义变体（EGTSP），通过比现有方法更强的约束传播实现计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束规划方法处理欧几里得TSP时，仅计算完整距离矩阵并将其视为一般图论问题，完全忽略了节点坐标中蕴含的几何信息，导致约束传播不够强、求解效率受限。

Method: 提出新的过滤算法，在约束逻辑编程中利用点的几何坐标信息实现更强的约束传播；并将该方法推广到欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP）等其他欧几里得TSP变体。

Result: 实验结果证明了所提出方法相比现有方法具有计算优势，能有效提升求解效率。

Conclusion: 利用几何信息可以显著增强TSP类问题的约束传播强度，这一方法论对欧几里得TSP及其变体（如EGTSP）均具有普适性，在智能车辆、智能交通等实际工程应用中具有潜力。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) is one of the best-known problems in computer science and arises in many engineering applications, such as smart vehicles and intelligent transportation systems. In the "Euclidean" case, each node is defined by its coordinates in the plane and distances are computed using the Euclidean metric. In the Constraint Programming (CP) literature, the Euclidean TSP is typically addressed by computing the full distance matrix and treating it as a general case; however this approach ignores the geometric information carried by the points' coordinates. In this work, we propose new filtering algorithms, implemented in Constraint Logic Programming (CLP), that exploit such geometric information to achieve stronger constraint propagation than existing approaches. Moreover, we show how this methodology can be extended to other Euclidean variants of the TSP, including the Euclidean Generalized Traveling Salesperson Problem (EGTSP), which is relevant in practical routing and logistics applications. Experimental results demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [276] [ComBodied Agents: a New Paradigm of Human-Centric Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10915)
*Qianggang Ding,Xingyao Wang,Rui Feng,Zhibin Wang,Feixiang Wang,Kelong Mao,Hao Sun,Zhiyao Luo,Jiankai Tang,Lei Li,Jiadong Guo,Minheng Ni,Weicong Lin,Chenxi Yang,Hongxiang Gao,Zhenghua Chen,Yang Bai,Min Wu,Jun Cheng,Huazhu Fu,Dacheng Tao,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Combodied Agents"（具身+数字融合智能体）这一以人为本的Agentic AI新范式。与传统数字智能体仅改变软件状态、具身智能体仅改变物理状态不同，Combodied Agents将人的持续演化状态与能动性作为建模、干预和评估的核心对象。系统通过多模态感知重建个人事件、纵向可修正记忆提供时间上下文、个人世界模型预测不同决策与干预下的未来状态，并通过可接受的干预策略在同意、不确定性、安全、可逆性和用户控制约束下提供适度支持，形成闭环。该方法使用有界、不确定感知、用户可修正的表征而非穷举式数字孪生，并提出场景中心评估、能动性保持指标、基准要求、边缘端个人模型及治理方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic AI存在结构性缺口：数字智能体只变换软件状态，具身智能体只变换物理状态，二者都无法理解人类遗忘、困惑、副作用或主动拒绝等深层原因，也无法判断何种支持最为恰当。老年患者漏服药物这一场景揭示，真正需要的是对人的状态本身进行建模、预测和干预，而非仅完成任务。

Method: 提出Combodied Agents框架：1）基于事件的多元模态感知——重建有意义的个人事件；2）纵向可修正记忆——提供时间上下文；3）个人世界模型——预测不同决策与干预下的未来个人状态与结果；4）可接受干预策略——在同意、不确定性、安全、可逆性与用户控制约束下选择适度支持。以人与环境反馈闭环更新系统，采用目的有界、不确定感知、用户可修正的表征，避免依赖穷举式Human Digital Twin。设计空间按人类状态目标、关系情境和智能体角色组织。

Result: 论文提出了统一的概念框架与设计空间，整合了个人助理、健康智能体、AI伴侣和自适应人机系统等分散能力为闭环系统。提出场景中心评估、能动性保持指标、基准要求、边缘原生个人模型和治理方向等研究纲领，将Agentic AI从外部任务完成转向持续的人类福祉。

Conclusion: Combodied Agents代表了Agentic AI的范式转变：从"完成任务"转向"维护人的福祉"，将人的状态轨迹而非工具结果作为智能体的核心目标对象，为下一代以人为本的自主智能系统提供了架构蓝图与研究议程。

Abstract: After an older adult misses a medication dose, a software agent can send another reminder and an embodied agent can bring the medication. Yet neither explains whether the person forgot, is confused, has side effects, or deliberately refused, nor what support is appropriate. This reveals a structural gap in Agentic AI: Digital Agents primarily transform software states, while Embodied Agents transform physical states; neither makes a person's evolving state and agency the primary object of modeling, intervention, and evaluation. We introduce Combodied Agents, a human-centered paradigm that perceives, models, predicts, and supports individual human-state trajectories over time, using software tools, sensors, wearables, robots, and human services as action channels rather than end goals. We unify fragmented capabilities across personal assistants, health agents, AI companions, and adaptive human--AI systems into a closed loop: event-based multimodal perception reconstructs meaningful personal events; longitudinal, correctable memory provides temporal context; Personal World Models estimate future personal states and outcomes under alternative decisions and interventions; and an admissible intervention policy selects proportionate support under consent, uncertainty, safety, reversibility, and user control. Feedback from the person and environment updates the loop. Rather than requiring an exhaustive Human Digital Twin, the framework uses purpose-bounded, uncertainty-aware, user-correctable representations. We organize the design space by human-state targets, relational contexts, and agent roles, and propose scenario-centered evaluation, agency-preservation metrics, benchmark requirements, edge-native personal models, and governance directions. Combodied Agents shift Agentic AI from external task completion toward sustained human benefit.

</details>


### [277] [IO Factory: Simulating AI-Enabled Influence Campaigns at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10920)
*Lukasz Olejnik,Wenchao Dong,Jonas R. Kunst,Signe Riemer-Sørensen,Tobias Herb,Meeyoung Cha,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IO Factory，一个用于模拟信息与影响力战役的AI驱动框架，将其建模为完整、可追溯的过程。该框架在受控模拟平台中整合了角色、平台行为、曝光记录、模型评估和群体信念变化，支持多达10万智能体的配置，为可复现研究和红队分析提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字操纵威胁已从单一语言模型的劝说性文本扩展到AI群体（AI swarms），即持续协调的智能体群，它们能适应平台反馈并将有组织的行动伪装成普通社交互动。这类战役无法从孤立消息中识别，必须在计划、平台行为、曝光、解读、测量和适应的连续频谱中分析，因此需要一个端到端的模拟框架。

Method: IO Factory在受控模拟平台中表示完整的信息战役过程，连接行为者角色、平台行为、曝光记录、基于结构化模型的评估以及模拟群体中的配置变化。框架通过记录每次运行中的行为者、目标、行动约束、曝光路径和测量规则，实现完整的可追溯性和可复现性。

Result: 作者实现了该架构并在多达10万智能体的配置下进行了评估。结果表明IO Factory能够按规模执行战役时间线，并产生可检查的曝光证据以及配置信念变量的测量变化。

Conclusion: IO Factory通过提供可复现的研究和红队分析工具，支持对协调性影响力行动的分析，弥补了传统方法无法从整体流程视角识别AI群体操纵的不足。

Abstract: We introduce IO Factory, an AI-driven framework for simulating information and influence campaigns as fully integrated, traceable processes. The threat of digital manipulation now extends beyond persuasive text from individual language models to AI swarms, i.e., persistent groups of coordinated agents that adapt to platform feedback and disguise organized campaigns as ordinary social interaction. Because such campaigns cannot be identified from isolated messages alone, they must be analyzed across a continuous spectrum of planning, platform action, exposure, interpretation, measurement, and adaptation. IO Factory represents this process inside a controlled simulated platform, linking actor roles, platform actions, exposure records, structured model-based evaluations, and configured changes in the simulated population. We implement the architecture and evaluate it across configurations of up to 100,000 agents. The results show that IO Factory executes campaign timelines at scale and produces inspectable evidence of exposure and measured movement in configured belief variables. By recording the actors, objectives, action constraints, exposure paths, and measurement rules used in each run, IO Factory supports reproducible research and red-team analysis of coordinated influence.

</details>


### [278] [ThinkRetrieve: Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning Traces for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10928)
*Vaibhav Singh,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Sarvesh Gharat,Soumyabrata Pal,Ramasuri Narayanam,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkRetrieve 是一个测试时扩展框架，通过在大型推理模型（LRMs）的推理轨迹中动态检索并注入已解决的示例，来增强逐步推理过程，从而克服传统顺序测试时扩展中收益递减的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型通过增加推理时计算量来提升性能，但近期研究表明顺序测试时扩展往往收益递减甚至为负，因为更长的推理轨迹会增加不确定性、错误累积和对原始问题的偏离。

Method: ThinkRetrieve 从外部问题语料库中，在推理的每个中间步骤动态检索相关的已解决示例，并将这些示例直接注入到思考轨迹中，为模型提供"如何推理"的指导，而不仅仅是"哪些事实相关"的信息。

Result: 在五个推理模型（1.5B-8B参数）上，针对 GSM-8K、MATH-500、AIME 2025 和 SciQ 数据集进行实验，ThinkRetrieve 持续优于标准测试时扩展方法，在 AIME 2025 上相对提升高达 60%。

Conclusion: 动态检索示例注入是一种有效的测试时扩展策略，能够改善大型推理模型在长链推理中的性能，缓解顺序扩展带来的收益递减问题。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by allocating additional inference-time compute to generate extended chain-of-thought reasoning. However, recent studies reveal that sequential test-time scaling often yields diminishing or even negative returns, as longer traces exhibit increased uncertainty, error compounding, and drift from the original problem. We propose ThinkRetrieve, a test-time scaling framework that augments the reasoning traces of LRMs with dynamically retrieved solved examples at each reasoning step. Given an external corpus of problems paired with step-by-step solutions, ThinkRetrieve retrieves relevant exemplars at each intermediate step and injects them directly into the thinking trace, providing the model with guidance on how to reason rather than merely what facts are relevant. Experiments across five reasoning models (1.5B--8B parameters) on GSM-8K, MATH-500, AIME 2025, and SciQ demonstrate that ThinkRetrieve consistently improves accuracy over standard test-time scaling, with relative gains of up to $60\%$ on AIME 2025.

</details>


### [279] [FedCGR: Federated Cross-Domain Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10929)
*Zhuodong Liu,Hugen Lv,Xiangyu Li,Bohan Guo,Peiyu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCGR，一种联邦生成式跨域推荐框架，通过将物品表示为基于公共元数据的离散语义ID序列来实现跨域对齐，并通过可靠性感知语义接口注入本地协同过滤信号和原型个性化生成器选择性聚合参数，以克服联邦环境下跨域对齐的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦跨域推荐中，跨域物品空间对齐依赖重叠用户和共享交互信号等行为锚点，但这些信号在客户端间稀疏、不可用或涉及隐私，导致跨域对齐困难。

Method: 1) 将物品表示为基于公共侧面元数据的离散语义ID(SID)序列，通过共享词汇表实现跨域对齐；2) 设计可靠性感知语义接口注入本地协同过滤证据；3) 训练原型个性化生成器，根据域相关性选择性聚合共享参数，保持域特定参数本地化。

Result: 在六个Amazon跨域场景中，FedCGR一致优于联邦生成式基线方法，并在全排序和采样评估协议下达到与强序列推荐和联邦CDR方法竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 通过稳定语义物品语言和显式适应性设计，FedCGR有效解决了联邦CDR中跨域对齐与隐私保护的矛盾，验证了生成式方法在联邦跨域推荐中的可行性。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) transfers preference knowledge across related domains, but federated deployment makes cross-domain alignment difficult because the behavioral anchors that align item spaces, such as overlapping users and shared interaction signals, are often sparse, unavailable, or privacy-sensitive across clients. To address this tension, we revisit federated CDR as generation over a stable semantic item language. By representing items as discrete semantic ID (SID) sequences derived from public item-side metadata, cross-domain item alignment is induced by a shared vocabulary rather than by exchanging private interactions or aligning domain-specific embeddings. Directly federating SID-based generators, however, introduces two design constraints: the SID tokenizer must remain fixed to preserve cross-client token consistency, which creates a semantic-only bottleneck because local collaborative filtering (CF) signals cannot be globally shared or aligned; meanwhile, standard federated averaging can cause negative transfer under domain heterogeneity. To overcome these constraints, we propose FedCGR, a federated generative CDR framework that keeps the item language stable and makes adaptation explicit. FedCGR injects local CF evidence through a reliability-aware semantic interface and trains a prototype-personalized generator that selectively aggregates shared parameters according to domain relatedness while keeping domain-specific quantities local. Experiments on six Amazon cross-domain scenarios show that FedCGR consistently outperforms federated generative baselines and achieves competitive performance against strong sequential and federated CDR methods under both full-ranking and sampled evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [280] [XCoT-VLA: Executable Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10976)
*Foundation Model Team,XPeng Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XCoT-VLA提出用紧凑的可执行CoT令牌替代冗长的自然语言推理链，用于自动驾驶的视觉-语言-动作模型，显著降低推理开销并提升轨迹生成精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型使用冗长的自然语言CoT进行场景理解和推理，但这对于实时驾驶控制而言存在开放性强、解码成本高、难以作为面向动作的表示进行优化等问题。

Method: 提出XCoT-VLA框架：(1) 从日志轨迹和场景上下文自动构建Reason-Action监督信号，学习紧凑的可执行CoT令牌；(2) 预测的XCoT序列保持在上下文中，通过共享多模态自注意力条件化固定轨迹查询；(3) 采用确定性令牌-函数路由，对XCoT令牌应用Reason FFN，对轨迹查询应用Control FFN进行流匹配轨迹生成；(4) 提出XCPO作为可选的可执行令牌空间内策略优化扩展。

Result: 在通用分布集合上纵向ADE从1.645降至1.323；在换道场景中横向FDE从1.616降至0.648。仅需2-6个可执行XCoT令牌即可表示驾驶推理，大幅降低自回归推理开销并保持在实时规划预算内。

Conclusion: 驾驶导向的推理可以做到紧凑、可执行，并直接连接到轨迹生成，为VLA模型在实时自动驾驶控制中的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can connect scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and trajectory generation for autonomous driving. However, verbose natural-language Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is poorly suited to real-time control because it is open-ended, costly to decode, and difficult to optimize as an action-facing representation. We propose XCoT-VLA, which replaces descriptive rationales with compact executable CoT tokens learned from automatically constructed Reason-Action supervision. Logged trajectories provide action evidence, while scene context supplies causal semantics. The predicted XCoT sequence remains in context and conditions fixed trajectory queries through shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token-function routing applies the Reason FFN to XCoT tokens and the Control FFN to trajectory queries for flow-matching trajectory generation. We further introduce XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) as an optional refinement extension in the same executable token space. XCoT-VLA reduces longitudinal ADE from 1.645 to 1.323 on a general-distribution set and lateral FDE from 1.616 to 0.648 in lane-change scenarios. By representing driving-oriented reasoning with only 2-6 executable XCoT tokens, our method substantially reduces autoregressive reasoning overhead and remains within the real-time planning budget. These results demonstrate that driving-oriented reasoning can be compact, executable, and directly connected to trajectory generation.

</details>


### [281] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen,Victoire Laurent,Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari,Lara Turgut,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出V-FiLLM框架，通过从可执行计算树生成金融推理基准，利用符号求值获得ground truth，实现任意规模、零标注成本的基准生成。探索了四个可独立控制的难度轴，并通过实验发现深度推理和数值扰动下准确率显著下降（最高降51%和47%），轻量LoRA微调可有效提升组合推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在STEM领域评估LLM取得进展，但金融结构化数据推理的研究相对不足。现有基准存在标注成本高、依赖模型生成导致错误率继承等问题，需要一种可扩展、正确性有保证的金融推理基准生成方法。

Method: V-FiLLM从真实表格构建可执行计算树，通过符号求值获得ground truth，将计算树渲染为自然语言问题，完全排除模型标注环节。框架设计了四个独立可控的难度维度：计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度、上下文规模。

Result: 实验发现：推理深度增加时准确率最高下降51%；对抗性数值扰动下最高下降47个百分点。轻量LoRA微调（基于验证的思维链轨迹）将保留问题上准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%，并在FinQA上超过基础模型5个百分点。

Conclusion: V-FiLLM提供了可扩展、正确性有保证的金融推理基准，揭示了LLM在金融表格推理中的关键弱点，并证明低成本、针对性的LoRA微调是提升组合推理能力的有效方向。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [282] [SkillZip: Evaluation-Free Skill Compression for Self-Evolving Agents by Discovering Reusable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11079)
*Xiaofan Bai,Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Yantao Zhang,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillZip 是一种免评估的技能压缩方法，通过寻找最短的忠实结构解释（typed MDL 目标）来压缩自进化智能体的技能，支持一次性模式和持续 Zip-on-Write 模式，在压缩性能、泛化性和成本开销上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体不断追加成功流程和失败修复，导致技能重复冗余、注入昂贵且难以维护。通用提示压缩不适用，因为技能不是扁平文本，包含名称/描述（何时适用）、工作流（如何执行）、工具/输出契约（有效性约束）及稀有异常。评估引导压缩虽有帮助，但引入 rollout、成本和评价集依赖。

Method: SkillZip 提出"解释一次，多处引用"的核心直觉：在适用范围处只陈述一次重复规则，将重复动作序列因子化为共享过程，只保留差异作为显式异常。形式化为带类型的极小描述长度（MDL）目标，包含技能契约和残差，受硬覆盖约束（覆盖每个触发条件、工作流边、工具需求、义务和输出字段）。提供简单共享阈值、天然保留稀有规则，支持高效局部更新。有一站式模式（单次结构化提取+确定性优化）和持续 Zip-on-Write 模式（整合补丁无需重放任务或重解析历史）。

Result: 通过全面实验评估，SkillZip 在压缩性能、泛化性和成本开销方面均表现出有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: SkillZip 通过免评估的结构化压缩方案，有效解决了自进化技能冗余问题，兼顾压缩率、忠实性和维护效率。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate reusable skills by appending successful procedures and failure fixes. Over time, the same requirement is often restated in several branches, examples, and warnings, while common action sequences are copied rather than reused. The resulting skill becomes expensive to inject and difficult to maintain. Generic prompt compression is ill-suited to this setting because a skill is not a flat passage: its name and description define when it applies, its workflow controls execution, its tool and output contracts constrain validity, and rare exceptions may remain essential even when no sampled task activates them. Evaluation-guided compression can test these behaviors, but it introduces rollouts, cost, and dependence on the compression-time evaluation set. We present SkillZip, an evaluation-free method that compresses a skill by finding its shortest faithful structural explanation. The intuition is explain once, reference many: state a repeated rule once at the scope where it applies, factor a repeated action sequence into a shared procedure, and keep only the differences as explicit exceptions. We formalize this intuition as a typed minimum description-length objective over a skill contract and a residual, subject to a hard coverage constraint for every extracted trigger, workflow edge, tool requirement, obligation, and output field. The formulation provides simple sharing thresholds, preserves unique rare rules by construction, and supports efficient local updates. SkillZip has a one-shot mode with one structured extraction call and deterministic optimization, and a continual Zip-on-Write mode that integrates each self-evolution patch without replaying tasks or reparsing the full history. Through comprehensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of SkillZip in compression performance, generalizability, and cost overhead.

</details>


### [283] [RTSKG: Building a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11080)
*Shutong Zhu,Tianxing Wu,Runfeng Liu,Yuang Gu,Xuan He,Yuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了铁路交通站点知识图谱（RTSKG）数据集，通过统一模式显式建模城市实体间的空间与语义交互，以支持城市级铁路交通站点相关任务，实验证明其在站点区域商店推荐和知识增强的客流预测中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市级铁路交通站点相关研究（如客流预测）依赖大规模城市数据，但往往忽略了各类城市实体之间复杂交互关系的数据组织问题，缺乏统一的结构化表示。

Method: 设计了RTSKG数据集，集成铁路交通站点、道路段、兴趣点（POI）等异构城市实体，通过专门设计的统一模式（schema）显式建模实体间的空间与语义交互，并以链接数据（Linked Data）形式在https://w3id.org/rtskg/公开发布。

Result: 在站点区域商店推荐和知识增强客流预测两个任务上的评估验证了RTSKG的有效性，展示了其支持城市级铁路交通站点分析的潜力。

Conclusion: RTSKG作为知识图谱数据集，通过显式建模城市实体间交互，为城市级铁路交通站点分析提供了有效的数据基础设施支撑。

Abstract: Rail transit systems play a vital role in urban mobility and economic development. As key components of such systems, rail transit stations function as critical transport hubs that enhance urban accessibility and stimulate development in surrounding areas. City-level rail transit station related tasks (e.g., ridership prediction) require large-scale urban data, but current studies often neglect complex interactions among various urban entities in terms of data organization. In this paper, to address the above issue, we build a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG) dataset which explicitly models the spatial and semantic interactions among different kinds of urban entities, to benefit city-level rail transit station related tasks. RTSKG integrates heterogeneous urban entities, such as rail transit stations, road segments, and points of interest, with a specially designed unified schema, and is accessible as Linked Data at https://w3id.org/rtskg/. Evaluations on station-area store recommendation and knowledge-enhanced ridership prediction demonstrate the effectiveness of RTSKG, highlighting its potential to support city-level rail transit station analysis.

</details>


### [284] [sLTN: Structural Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11136)
*Davide Rinaldi,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出sLTN，一个扩展逻辑张量网络（LTN）的神经符号框架，通过将结构维度（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）作为语言中的一等公民，使框架能够显式表达时序、顺序和关系约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始LTN框架主要适用于平坦的个体数据集合，无法显式捕捉诸如时间顺序、序列位置或图连通性等结构组织信息。

Method: 引入结构维度作为命名张量轴，支持显式量化、结构关系表达，并形式化定义了语法和模糊张量语义；同时提供了基于PyTorch的声明式实现。

Result: 证明了在无结构维度时，sLTN可退化为原始LTN语义；并在时序和序列推理示例中进行展示。

Conclusion: sLTN作为LTN的自然扩展，将结构信息融入逻辑层面，为时序、序列和图结构数据的神经符号推理提供了更强大的表达能力。

Abstract: Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) provide a neurosymbolic framework in which first-order logic is interpreted through tensor operations, enabling logical constraints to be integrated with differentiable learning. However, the original formulation of LTN is primarily suited to data represented as flat collections of individuals, and does not explicitly capture structural organization such as temporal order, sequential position, or graph connectivity. We introduce sLTN, an extension of LTN that makes structural dimensions first-class elements of the language. Structural dimensions represent named tensor axes associated with domain-specific organization, such as time steps, sequence positions, or graph nodes. They can be quantified explicitly, related through structural relations, and used to express temporal, sequential, and relational constraints directly at the logical level. We formalize the syntax and fuzzy tensor semantics of sLTN and show that, in the absence of structural dimensions, the framework recovers the original LTN semantics as a special case. We further describe a PyTorch implementation based on a declarative signature, formula parsing, and tensorial interpretation. The framework is illustrated on representative temporal and sequential reasoning examples. This paper serves as a companion to the sltn library, available at https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn.

</details>


### [285] [Long-Horizon AI Research for Grothendieck Constant: A Case Study in Human-AI Mathematical Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11195)
*Alan Li,Rahul Saha,Anton Xue,Swarat Chaudhuri,Adam Klivans,Pravesh K Kothari,Raghu Meka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个详细的案例研究，展示了AI如何被用于改善Grothendieck常数$K_G$的上下界，将最佳已知界收紧到$\frac{6\pi}{11} \le K_G \le \frac{\pi}{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}$。这些改进是由一个AI研究系统实现的，其结论被领域专家认定为新颖见解。论文详细讨论了将AI用于数学研究的经验，包括其优缺点以及为AI创造突破性见解所需的理想条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在数学研究中日益普及，但如何有效使用它们仍然不清楚。作者希望通过一个具体的案例研究来阐明AI在数学研究中的实际应用价值、优势和局限性。

Method: 采用案例研究的方法，展示一个AI研究系统如何成功改进Grothendieck常数的已知界限，并系统性地分析使用AI进行数学研究的全过程经验，包括创建有利于AI产生突破性见解的理想条件。

Result: 成功将Grothendieck常数的界限收紧至$\frac{6\pi}{11} \le K_G \le \frac{\pi}{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}$，且这些改进被领域专家认定为新颖见解，证明了AI在数学研究中可以产生真正的突破性贡献。

Conclusion: AI确实能够在数学研究中产生被专家认可的新颖突破，但其有效使用需要仔细设计理想条件。总结AI在数学研究中的优缺点，为未来的AI辅助数学研究提供宝贵经验。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used in mathematics research, but it is often unclear how to use them effectively. Towards this, we present an extensive case study of how AI was used to improve bounds on the Grothendieck constant $K_G$, which captures the hardness between combinatorial problems and their continuous relaxations. Specifically, while the precise value of $K_G$ is not known, we recently tightened the best known bounds to \[
  \frac{6π}{11}
  \;\le\;
  K_G
  \;\le\;
  \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}. \] Crucially, these improvements were achieved using an AI research system that could arrive at insights deemed novel by domain experts. We give a detailed discussion of our experience using AI for mathematics research, particularly touching upon its strengths and weaknesses, as well as our experience with creating ideal conditions for AI to arrive at breakthrough insights.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [286] [Ollivier's Ricci Curvature on Complex-weighted Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10132)
*Yu Tian,Eleanor Wiesler,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将Ollivier Ricci曲率推广到复权图（包括有向图）的新方法，建立了与磁拉普拉斯算子的关系及组合上下界，并展示了其在有向网络社区检测中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散Ricci曲率公式主要局限于无向网络和实值权重，无法处理现实应用中有向及复权关系（如社交、生物、量子及信号处理网络）的曲率分析需求。

Method: 将Ollivier Ricci曲率做原则性扩展以适用于复权图，将无权有向图作为特例包含在内；建立了新概念与磁拉普拉斯算子的联系，推导了与局部邻域环结构相关的组合上下界，并开发了曲率估计的计算方法。

Result: 提出了适用于复权图（涵盖有向图）的扩展Ricci曲率定义，证明了其理论性质（磁拉普拉斯关联、组合界），并通过有向网络社区检测实验验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了Ricci曲率在复杂网络分析中的适用范围，使其能够处理方向性和复权重的网络结构，为社区检测等下游任务提供了新的几何工具。

Abstract: Understanding the geometry of complex networks is critical for effective modeling and analysis across domains. While discrete notions of Ricci curvature have emerged as powerful tools for characterizing both local and global network structure, existing formulations are largely confined to undirected networks with real-valued weights. This limits the use of curvature-based analysis of directional and complex-weighted relations that arise naturally in many applications, from social and biological systems to quantum and signal-processing networks. In this work, we introduce a principled extension of Ollivier's Ricci curvature to complex-weighted graphs, which encompasses directed graphs as a special case. We establish fundamental theoretical properties of this new notion, including relations to the magnetic Laplacian and combinatorial upper and lower bounds that relate curvature to cycle structure in local neighborhoods. We further develop computational methods for curvature estimation and demonstrate their utility in community detection on directed networks.

</details>


### [287] [FUBU-EPSTEIN: A Large-Scale Twitter Dataset on the Jeffrey Epstein Case and Its Global Public Discourse (2019-2023)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10210)
*Michael Kreil,Tristan Manfred Stöber,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了FUBU-EPSTEIN数据集，这是一个包含5438万条推文、711万用户的大规模多维研究语料库，用于计算性研究Jeffrey Epstein案引发的全球数字平台话语动态，包括注意力冲击、阴谋论和归责。数据集以脱敏、去文本化的形式公开，并配有丰富的注释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jeffrey Epstein案在数字平台上引发了复杂而长期的全球话语，具有注意力冲击、阴谋论和归责等动态特征，但目前缺乏支持此类计算研究的大规模、多维数据集。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 在2019年8月至2023年4月期间近实时连续采集Twitter状态；(2) 构建3703万条有向社交联系图；(3) 使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct对情感、阴谋与错误信息立场、毒性、道德情感等维度进行注释；(4) 创建去文本化、去标识化的公开衍生数据集，保留去重后的状态、注释、粗粒度时间信息和4615万条内部状态关系。

Result: 生成了5438万条推文、711万用户、3703万条社交关系边和4615万条内部状态关系的大规模注释数据集，同时通过去除文本、标识符和精确时间戳等措施最大限度降低隐私泄露和平台内容再分发风险。

Conclusion: 该数据集在降低泄露和内容再分发风险的同时，支持纵向内容和扩散分析，为研究长期社会话语动态提供了宝贵资源；原始数据可通过受控方式供合作研究使用。

Abstract: The criminal case of Jeffrey Epstein has generated a complex, long-running global discourse on digital platforms, characterized by punctuated attention shocks, conspiracy theories, and blame attribution. To facilitate the computational study of these dynamics, we introduce the FUBU-EPSTEIN dataset, a large-scale, multi-dimensional research corpus of 54.38 million Twitter statuses authored by 7.11 million users and collected between August 2019 and April 2023. The source corpus was captured continuously in near-real time and enriched with a directed social contact graph of 37.03 million edge rows and annotations from Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct covering sentiment, conspiracy and misinformation stance, toxicity, moral emotion, and related dimensions. For public distribution, we created a textless, de-identified derivative that retains one row for every deduplicated status, categorical and numeric annotations, coarse temporal information, and 46.15 million internal status relationships. It excludes tweet text, original post and user identifiers, handles, profile fields, exact timestamps, and reverse mappings. The resulting release supports longitudinal content and diffusion analyses while reducing disclosure and platform-content redistribution risks. To request access to raw data for collaborative research under ethical and legal safeguards, contact fubu.dataset@gmail.com.

</details>


### [288] [A Graph Approach to the Academic Publishing Network: A Heterogeneous Model and Structural Screening over OpenAlex Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10774)
*Robert Šamárek,Radek Martinek*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于异质图模型的学术出版诚信分析方法，在OpenAlex开放数据上构建包含七种节点和七种边类型的图模型，结合投影、社区检测和三种异常筛查检测器，并以VSB技术大学和Scopus/DOAJ剔除期刊为语料进行验证，发布了开源库apnet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学计量学依赖孤立的表格化指标（如h-index、影响因子），忽视了拓扑上下文信息，无法捕捉协调性的不正当出版行为（如引文操纵、合著互惠等）。需要一个能利用图结构信息来检测异常出版模式的系统性方法。

Method: 构建异质多元图模型（OpenAlex数据，7种节点、7种边类型）；基于投影方法（引文网络和合著网络）进行分析；使用可解释的结构指标、社区检测以及三种异常出版模式筛查检测器（密集合著团、局部闭合引文环、主题孤立期刊）；明确避免二元分类，检测器返回带结构证据的排序候选供人工评估。

Result: 在VSB技术大学语料上，社区检测恢复了真实研究组，中心性识别出跨学科桥梁，筛查器标记了异常模式。在期刊中心语料上，发现朴素病例对照设计会产生虚假的显著性信号（知名度的混杂效应）；匹配后仅学科范围广度是稳健信号（AUC 0.70）；基于图的PageRank声望指标在抗引文游戏操纵方面比计数型指标高一个数量级。

Conclusion: 图分析方法能有效补充传统科学计量学指标，为出版诚信监测提供可解释的、抗操纵的结构化证据；方法以开源库apnet发布，可在普通硬件上分钟级运行，具有实用推广价值。

Abstract: The academic publishing ecosystem is a vast, heterogeneous network of works, authors, institutions, journals, and topics. Traditional scientometrics reduces it to isolated tabular indicators (h-index, Impact Factor) that ignore topological context and are not designed to capture coordinated illegitimate practices. Building on our companion review, which proposed graph analysis of publishing integrity, this paper implements that approach. We define a heterogeneous multivariate graph model over OpenAlex open data (seven node types, seven edge types) and a methodology based on projections (citation and co-authorship networks), interpretable structural metrics, community detection, and three screening detectors of anomalous publishing patterns. We deliberately avoid binary classification: detectors return ranked candidates with explicit structural evidence for human assessment. On the institutional corpus of VSB - Technical University of Ostrava (2020-2025) with its one-hop citation neighbourhood, community detection recovers real research groups, centralities identify cross-disciplinary bridges, and the screenings flag dense co-authorship cliques, locally closed citation loops, and thematically isolated venues. On a second, venue-centric corpus with external ground truth (journals delisted by Scopus and DOAJ) and size-matched controls, a naive case-control design yields seemingly strong but spurious detectors (a prominence confound), whereas after matching the only robust signal is the breadth of disciplinary scope (AUC 0.70); an open graph-based prestige measure (PageRank over the journal citation network) tracks a JIF proxy while being an order of magnitude more resistant to citation gaming than count-based indicators. We release the method as the open-source library apnet with a reproducible CLI workflow and a web interface; the analysis runs on commodity hardware in minutes.

</details>


### [289] [Shaping the notion of #wellbeing in the therapy culture context: an analysis through Instagram narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10793)
*Ainara Larrondo-Ureta,Simón Peña-Fernández,Jordi Morales-i-Gras*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究通过大数据分析技术，对Instagram上#wellbeing标签下的9,844条帖子进行数据挖掘、文本分析、计算机视觉分析和性别检测，揭示了该平台上幸福感话语以女性化为主导，聚焦于心理、精神和灵性层面，其中治疗类和积极心理学叙事最具参与度，而身体活动和营养相对次要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统地识别Instagram上#wellbeing内容中的文本和视觉主题，评估其参与度差异，并调查视觉叙事中存在的性别偏见，以更全面地理解社交媒体上幸福感话语的构成与传播特征。

Method: 采用多种大数据分析技术：首先对9,844条帖子进行数据挖掘和文本分析以识别和分类关键标签，通过层次聚类将帖子归入主题群；其次使用非参数统计检验评估参与度；再次利用计算机视觉模型分析图像叙事并按视觉相似性分组；最后使用目标检测模型检验图像中的性别表征。

Result: 研究揭示#wellbeing话语在Instagram上以女性化为主导，主要聚焦于心理、精神和灵性方面。治疗类和积极心理学叙事最为普遍且参与度最高，身体活动和营养仅扮演次要角色。研究识别出两大宏观叙事：一是心理健康与情绪幸福感（涉及治疗、自我发现、灵性和励志语录），二是身体活动和健康习惯（侧重运动与营养）。

Conclusion: 本研究系统揭示了Instagram平台上幸福感话语的主题结构和性别特征，表明幸福感叙事显著偏向女性化和心理健康维度，为理解社交媒体上健康与幸福感传播的现状提供了实证基础。

Abstract: The aim of this paper is to (1) identify textual and visual themes and sub-themes associated with the #wellbeing hashtag on Instagram, (2) assess their varying levels of engagement and (3) investigate gender bias present in the analysed visual narratives. This study employs a range of big data analysis techniques to investigate various dimensions of wellbeing on Instagram. Initially, a sample of 9,844 posts was processed using data mining and text analysis methods to identify and categorise significant hashtags, which facilitated the classification of posts into thematic clusters through hierarchical clustering. Engagement was then assessed using non-parametric statistical tests. Additionally, computer vision models were used to analyse and classify visual narratives, grouping images into communities based on visual similarities. Finally, gender representation in the images was examined using object detection models. The study reveals that the discourse around #wellbeing on Instagram is predominantly feminised and focuses primarily on mental, psychological and spiritual aspects. Therapeutic and positive psychology narratives are the most prevalent and engaging, while physical activity and nutrition play a relatively secondary role. Two major macro-narratives emerge: firstly, mental health and emotional wellbeing, often featuring hashtags related to therapy, self-discovery, spirituality and motivational quotes; secondly, though less prominently, themes and visual narratives concerning physical activity and healthy habits, emphasising exercise and nutrition.

</details>


### [290] [MARS: A framework for modelling register-based social networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10946)
*Katherine Hamilton,Irina Epure,Frank Takes*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARS（Multiplex Affiliation-based Random Spatially-embedded）图框架，用于建模基于注册数据的社会网络，并在荷兰全国规模的注册社交网络上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于注册的社会网络因生成过程复杂，现有的随机图模型无法有效地进行结构分析，阻碍了潜在社会系统中有意义的洞察发现。

Method: 提出MARS图框架，复制注册社会网络的构建方法，推导其通用及特殊情况下的基本统计性质，并实现一个简单模型进行验证。

Result: 该简单模型能够恢复与荷兰全国规模注册社会网络相似的性质；同时分析了空间联系强度对网络闭合的影响，发现空间自由度增加与社会凝聚力下降相关。

Conclusion: MARS框架能有效建模注册社会网络，并揭示了空间自由与社会凝聚力的负相关关系，验证了与现有实证发现的一致性。

Abstract: Register-based social networks have become of increasing interest in countries where formal government-curated microdata is available. Due to the non-trivial generative process of register-based networks, existing random graph models fail to facilitate effective structural analysis, hindering the discovery of meaningful insights in the underlying social system. In this paper we introduce the Multiplex Affiliation-based Random Spatially-embedded (MARS) graph framework, which replicates the construction method of register-based social networks. We derive fundamental statistical properties of MARS ensembles in general and special cases. To demonstrate the applicability of the framework, we implement a simple model under the MARS framework and show that it recovers similar properties to those exhibited by the population-scale register-based social network of the Netherlands. Furthermore, we analyse the effect of spatial tie strength on closure in the network and compare our results with existing empirical findings, showing that increased spatial freedom is correlated with decreased social cohesion.

</details>
